| summary_finite re-assembles the theorem from the fixed records. (i) For every FiniteRealDifferentialDatum D on (A, tau) (tau a FaithfulTracialState) and orthonormal bases, the Hodge packet ofDatum D tau b0 b1 (all packet laws derived from the datum, see thm:finite-Hodge-Dirac) satisfies IsRealEvenKO0Triple (faithful pi, D = D*, grading axioms, antiunitary J with J^2 = I, JD = DJ, J gamma = gamma J, opposite representation, order zero and first order). Functoriality: a DatumIso (a *-isomorphism phi : A ≃⋆ₐ A' with tau' o phi = tau, and a linear isometry u : K -> K' with u(a delta(b)) = phi(a) delta'(phi b) on the generators of the retained bimodule) induces, with everything derived (phi tensor phi on Omega_u^1, u(T_delta w) = T_delta'((phi tensor phi) w), surjectivity onto Ran T_delta'), unitaries U0 on L^2 and U1 on H1 and the IsometricTransport ofDatumTransport into the pull-back packet comap phi; U intertwines pi, D, gamma, J and pi^o (and B_a), U(id) = 1 and U(G o F) = U(G) U(F) (ofDatumTransport_refl/_comp). (ii) Scale: for delta != 0, s > 0, s != 1, the datum s delta on the same (A, tau) has Dirac operator s D != D (ofDatum_scale_dirac, ofDatum_scale_dirac_ne; full prop:no-scale content in no_scale). Commutant clause: for every marked finite triple, X is a compatible implementer iff X - D lies in c_S (summary_finite_marked, from commutant_fibre). (iii) summary_finite_marked: injective commutant coordinates with exactly q_S components recovering the triple, sharpness, and the three essential-image lines with Toeplitz strictness (line 3 in the screen form of sqd, accepted by the coordinator on 2 Oct 2026 under the reading 'symmetric = self-adjoint'; see thm:essential-image). Rendering: 'differential-preserving *-isomorphism' is formalized as DatumIso (a *-isomorphism plus an isometry of the bimodules carrying generators a delta(b) to phi(a) delta'(phi b)); bases of the target packet are arbitrary (same index types, as forced by the derived unitarity). Checklist: (b) the triple and the transport are built from data and their laws derived; (a) the scale clause comes from the derived scaling of the datum (no defined Scale.dirac). |

Scoped: the mesh sequence is indexed by d = n + 1 (eventually d >= 3); the covariance identity and the distance theorem are separate Lean statements linked through the common Space inner product rather than one bundled lemma. |

For fixed connected (G,m,c), c > 0 (IsConnectedIncidence B, derived from graph connectedness by isConnectedIncidence_incidenceReal): the affinity map J(G,c) -> H^1(G;R), j -> [artanh(j/c)], is a C^infinity diffeomorphism from the open fibre in Ker B onto H^1 (affinityPartialHomeomorph: OpenPartialHomeomorph with source the fibre and target all of H^1, ContDiffOn on the source, ContDiff inverse; derivative a linear isomorphism). For a representative a0 of alpha, the log-cosh functional has a unique mean-zero minimiser phi_alpha (strictly convex, coercive), a_alpha = a0 + B^T phi_alpha = artanh(j_alpha/c), j_alpha = c tanh a_alpha = 𝔄^{-1}(alpha), and the complete directed generator k = (c + j_alpha)/m_tail (forwardRate) is given explicitly and shared by every fibre current of class alpha. |

under integrability of lambda^{-1} against \|mu\| (finite static inverse moment) the memory cutoff RG_Lambda preserves the zero-energy head function F_{A,mu}(0) (dynamicFunction_rg_zero, eq:supp-memory-static-preserved); for surjective q, a unique rhobar with rho = rhobar o q exists iff ker q <= ker rho (monodromy_descends_iff); and coarse grainings compose strictly for composable quotients, nested thresholds Lambda_2 <= Lambda_1 and nested isometries W_1 W_2 (CoarseGraining.comp, image_comp, using rg_rg). Scoped: F_{A,mu}(0) is the dynamic function evaluated at 0 under the integrability hypothesis (the paper's limit from the negative axis is not separately formalized); graphs are finite oriented with positive conductances; the effective space is finite-dimensional (Matrix H H C). |

carrierReverse_class). left0/right0/j0 are the matrices <b_i, f b_j> of L_a, R_a, a -> a*; left1/right1/j1 those of lambda(a), rho(a), C restricted to Ran T_delta; differential and B a those of partial and B_a b = rho(b) delta(a). No packet field is assumed: star-adjointness (tau tracial for R_a), commuting actions, the Leibniz block (from delta Leibniz), right-linearity of B_a (right_mul), B_unit (right_one), J^2 = I (involutivity of * and C), J0 L_a = R_{a*} J0, J1 lambda(a) = rho(a*) J1, J1 partial = partial J0 (C delta = delta o *), and J0 partial* = partial* J1 (derived via adjoint and antiunitarity) are all theorems, via generic lemmas coordMat_comp_linear / coordMat_comp_antilinear / coordMat_of_adjoint. finite_hodge_dirac then states for ofDatum: pi faithful (ofDatum_representation_injective), D Hermitian (finite-dimensional, so compact resolvent and bounded commutators are automatic), gamma* = gamma, gamma^2 = I, [gamma, pi(a)] = 0, D gamma = -gamma D, J antiunitary (unitary coordinate matrix), J^2 = I, JD = DJ, J gamma = gamma J, J pi(b*) J^-1 = pi^o(b) with pi^o(b) = R_b + rho(b) (ofDatum_oppositeRepresentation), order zero and first order, eq:finite-commutator, and eq:finite-Lip (\|\|[D,pi(a)]\|\| = \|\|B_a\|\| and, for a = a*, \|\|[D,pi(a)]\|\| = 0 iff partial a = 0). ofDatum_dirac identifies dirac with the matrix of [[0, partial*],[partial, 0]] with partial* = LinearMap.adjoint. Scoped: A carries [FiniteDimensional C A] as a typeclass (it is derivable from the datum's basis: FiniteRealDifferentialDatum.finiteDimensional_algebra); operators are represented by their matrices in arbitrary orthonormal bases (statement holds for every choice). Non-vacuity: exists_matrix_datum gives a datum with delta != 0 on M_2(C) with the normalized trace (inner derivation [diag(i,-i), .]), and a closing `example` instantiates finite_hodge_dirac/hodge_zero_mode/no_scale on it. Checklist: (b) fields derived from the datum; (k) non-trivial instance M_2(C). |

| FiniteRealDifferentialDatum.scale D s is the datum s*delta (same algebra, bimodule, actions and C; the derivation axioms are re-proved, star compatibility using s real). Derived, not defined: T_{s delta} = s T_delta, Ker T_{s delta} = Ker T_delta and Ran T_{s delta} = Ran T_delta for s != 0, Gram Q_{s delta} = s^2 Q_delta; the class identification Omega/Ker -> Omega/Ker transported to the range carriers is s^{-1} id (scaleClassIdentification_apply), and the canonical unitary (s times it, [omega] -> s[omega]) is isometric for s^2 Q_delta vs Q_delta and sends partial_{s delta} a to s partial_delta a. With the corresponding basis scaleBasis, the Hodge packet ofDatum (D.scale s) has every block equal to that of D except differential -> s differential and B_a -> s B_a, hence D -> sD (ofDatum_scale_dirac), L_D(a) = \|\|[D,pi(a)]\|\| -> s L_D(a) for every a (ofDatum_scale_lipschitz), and the attained Connes distance scales by s^{-1} (ofDatum_scale_connesDistance, 'whenever finite' = the supremum is attained). no_scale bundles these for delta != 0, s > 0, and adds the non-determination: tau and A are unchanged while some self-adjoint a has L_D(a) > 0 and L_{sD}(a) != L_D(a) for s != 1; ofDatum_scale_dirac_ne gives sD != D. Checklist: (a) the carrier identification and its s^{-1}/s factors are derived from T_{s delta} = s T_delta; the delta != 0 non-determination clause is now proved; non-vacuity: the M_2(C) example applies ofDatum_scale_dirac_ne with s = 2. |
| `prop:finite-CQMS` | proposition | — | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.finite_cqms_matrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.finite_cqms_matrix_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.tendsto_iff_forall_tendsto_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.finite_cqms_matrix_leibniz_star`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.finite_cqms_matrix_converse_not_bddAbove`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.finite_cqms_matrix_converse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.isState_of_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.isState_vectorState`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.kernelConstants`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.lipData`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.selfAdjointPart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricMatrixExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.finite_compact_quantum_metric`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteCompactQuantumMetric.lipD_mul_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteCompactQuantumMetricExact.lean) | Proved, with the following disclosed scoping. The finite algebra is realised as a *-subalgebra S of Matrix H H C (H finite, nonempty) with the inclusion as (faithful) representation, so the paper's \|\|a\|\| is the Euclidean operator norm; D is any matrix (Hermitian for the star identity); the kernel hypothesis Ker partial = C1 is taken in the form: a in S with [D,a] = 0 is a complex multiple of 1 (this is thm:finite-spectralization's characterisation, cf. FiniteRealEvenSpectralizationExact.Packet.connesMetricFinite_iff_kernelConstants). The real order-unit space A_sa is selfAdjointPart S, L_D = lipSA D S = \|\|[D, a]\|\|, tau = normalized trace; the abstract package LipData is instantiated (lipData) with continuity of L_D proved, and the kernel condition transported to A_sa (kernelConstants: the scalar is real). finite_cqms_matrix gives the Poincare-Lip inequality, finiteness of mk_{L_D} on states with mk <= C\|\|phi - psi\|\| <= 2C, \|phi(a) - psi(a)\| <= L_D(a) mk, and the bi-Lipschitz comparison with the dual norm; finite_cqms_matrix_tendsto: mk-convergence of states iff norm convergence, and tendsto_iff_forall_tendsto_apply: norm convergence iff pointwise (weak-*) convergence in the finite-dimensional dual, so mk_{L_D} induces the weak-* topology. States are modelled as normalized contractive functionals on A_sa (LipData.IsState); isState_of_nonneg proves that every positive normalized functional (phi(1) = 1, phi >= 0 on positive semidefinite elements) is such a state, so the paper's state space S(A) is covered (the converse inclusion, standard in C*-algebras, is not needed for the claims and not proved). Leibniz and star identities: finite_cqms_matrix_leibniz_star. Converse: if Ker partial contains a non-scalar self-adjoint element, two (genuinely positive) vector states have unbounded supremum eq:finite-state-metric (finite_cqms_matrix_converse_not_bddAbove), i.e. infinite distance. |

The hypotheses of the old coordinate lemma are now derived: differential *v unitVector = 0 from delta(1) = 0 (hodgeDifferential_one: partial 1 = 0 in H1), unitVector != 0 from 1 != 0 (tau(1) = 1). The commutant-ambiguity sentence is thm:commutant-fibre (proved, commutant_fibre). Depends on thm:finite-Hodge-Dirac (proved in this batch). Checklist: (b) instantiated at the datum level, (l) the unit hypothesis is no longer assumed; non-vacuity via the M_2(C) example. |

| Proved concretely for the marked finite spectral triples of def:marked-spectral-triple (MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple: H = C^K in an orthonormal basis, operators = complex K x K matrices, real Hilbert-Schmidt inner product hsInner X Y = Re tr(X^H Y) realised on the type synonym HSMatrix K). IsCompatibleImplementer S X = X Hermitian, X gamma + gamma X = 0 if gamma is declared, J X = eps' X J if J is declared, and [X, pi(a)] = [D, pi(a)] for all a. commutant_fibre bundles: (eq:Dirac-affine) IsCompatibleImplementer S X <-> exists C in c_S with X = D + C, c_S = commutantSpace S given concretely by mem_commutantSpace_iff (eq:commutant-space); D_der = centeredDirac S = D - P_c D is compatible and is the unique minimum-HS-norm point (hsNorm_centeredDirac_le, hsNorm_eq_centeredDirac_iff); for EVERY real HS-orthonormal family C_1..C_{q_S} in c_S with q_S = commutantDim S = dim_R c_S, D = D_der + sum_j <C_j, D>_HS C_j (eq:commutant-coordinates/eq:commutant-reconstruction; the general form for every compatible X is eq_centeredDirac_add_sum) and a compatible implementer with the same q_S coordinates equals D (eq_dirac_of_hsInner_eq); such families exist (exists_orthonormal_commutant_family); any injective real linear coordinate map alpha : c_S -> R^m applied to P_c X recovers D (eq_dirac_of_commutantCoordinates_eq), while for m < q_S no alpha is injective and some compatible X != D has the same coordinates (not_injective_of_lt_commutantDim, exists_ne_of_lt_commutantDim; the paper says 'in general', the Lean proves it always); the derivation-only reconstruction is exactly the centered class: compatible X equals D_der iff P_c X = 0, and D = D_der iff P_c D = 0 (eq_centeredDirac_iff, centeredDirac_eq_dirac_iff). P_c is the genuine real HS orthogonal projection (hsInner_sub_commutantProjection, commutantProjection_eq_self). The concrete statements are obtained from new abstract lemmas in the same file (orthonormal-basis expansion of the anchor projection, recovery, sharpness) on top of DerivationOnlyAustereImage via isCompatibleImplementer_iff (concrete fibre = abstract SameMarkedDerivation fibre) and commutantDim_eq_anchorRank. Renderings: J is a conjugate-linear antiunitary on C^K with declared sign data; marks are declared operator families (see def:marked-spectral-triple). Non-vacuity: trivialMarkedFiniteSpectralTriple and exists_orthonormal_commutant_family. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `prop:finite-calibrated-recovery` | proposition | Finite recovery from commutant coordinates | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.derivation_only_austere_image`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.compatibleAnchor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.centeredRepresentative`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.centered_sameMarkedDerivation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.orthogonal_projection_eq_centered`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.complete_solution_fibre`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.anchorRank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.sharpAnchorCoordinates_injective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.anchor_count_lower_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivationOnlyAustereImage.fewer_anchors_not_injective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DerivationOnlyAustereImage.lean) | Proved for the concrete marked finite spectral triples of def:marked-spectral-triple. MarkedCommutantCoordinates.MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple.finite_calibrated_recovery: if S' has the same represented algebra, grading, real structure, marks and commutator derivation as S (SameCommutatorData), then S'_der = S_der, i.e. the commutator data determine D_der. Equal values of the q_S orthonormal HS coordinates, or of any injective real linear commutant-coordinate map, force S' = S. finite_calibrated_recovery_sharp: for every m < q_S and every m-component coordinate map, some S' ≠ S has the same commutator data and the same coordinates, so q_S coordinates are necessary in every case. The concrete results are transported from the abstract finite-dimensional real inner-product model of DerivationOnlyAustereImage (compatibleAnchor, centeredRepresentative, orthogonal_projection_eq_centered, complete_solution_fibre, sharpAnchorCoordinates_injective, anchor_count_lower_bound, fewer_anchors_not_injective). The transport uses isCompatibleImplementer_iff (concrete compatible implementers = abstract SameMarkedDerivation fibre) and commutantDim_eq_anchorRank (q_S = abstract anchor rank). |

/../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SpectralCompressionsSequential.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.spectrallyQuasidiagonal_proper`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitzTriple`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitzTriple_not_spectrallyQuasidiagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitzTriple_one_le_norm_commutator`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitz_separableSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitz_numberOperator_symmetric`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitz_numberResolvent_isStarNormal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitz_commutatorBounded_shift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.toeplitz_commutatorBounded_star_shift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ToeplitzScreenObstruction.commutatorBoundedStarSubalgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/Certificates/ToeplitzSpectralTripleExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.every_compactResolventSpectralTriple_is_strongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimit`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.StrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimit`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.tendsto_finiteSpectralCompression_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.tendsto_finiteSpectralCompression_multiplicativityDefect_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompactResolventStrongCofinalMarkedRenewalLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SpectralCompression.normMultiplicativeAlong_iff_quasidiagonalAlong`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SpectralCompressionStrictQuasidiagonalEquivalenceExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SpectralCompression.compress_mul_sub_mul_compress`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SpectralCompressionMultiplicativityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SpectralCompression.norm_compress_star_mul_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SpectralCompressionMultiplicativityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SpectralCompression.norm_compress_mul_star_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SpectralCompressionMultiplicativityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SpectralizationEssentialImageTrichotomyExact.toeplitz_strong_not_strict`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SpectralizationEssentialImageTrichotomyExact.lean) | Proved for the NCG.SpectralTriple encoding of compact-resolvent triples (A_D = smoothAlgebra) on a separable Hilbert space. (1) spectral_compressions_strong: a sequence P_n = diracSpectralScreen S (s n) of finite-rank orthogonal spectral projections of D (onto finitely many eigenspaces, via the compact normal resolvent), increasing (P_{n+1}P_n = P_n, ranges increase), preserving dom D and commuting with D, with P_n -> I strongly, P_n T P_n -> T strongly for EVERY bounded T (in particular pi(a), the bounded extension of [D,pi(a)] and the resolvents) and product defects P_nTP_nUP_n - P_nTUP_n -> 0 strongly (eq:strong-compression-topology); the sequence is extracted from the Finset-indexed screen net using a dense sequence (exists_monotone_screens). (2) compressed_commutator_identity (eq:compressed-commutator-identity): for x in P H, P D (P pi(a) x) - P pi(a) P D x = P (D pi(a) x - pi(a) D x) (and = P C x for the bounded extension C, compressed_commutator_identity_of_extension). (3) normMultiplicative_iff_commutator_tendsto: along any screen sequence, \|\|P_n pi(ab) P_n - P_n pi(a) P_n pi(b) P_n\|\| -> 0 for all a,b in A_D iff \|\|[P_n, pi(a)]\|\| -> 0 for all a in A_D (general C*-algebra lemma normMultiplicativeOn_iff_commutatorDecayOn on star-closed subsets, commutator <-> corners). (4) hasNormMultiplicativeCompression_iff_spectrallyQuasidiagonal. (5) Properness: toeplitzTriple is an NCG.SpectralTriple on the separable l^2(N) (number operator: symmetric, normal compact resolvent; A_D = StarAlgebra.adjoin {S}, all of whose elements have bounded commutators since [N,S] = S, [N,S*] = -S* and the bounded-commutator operators form a star subalgebra) which is not spectrally quasidiagonal (every nonzero finite spectral screen has \|\|[P,S]\|\| >= 1), packaged in spectrallyQuasidiagonal_proper; it is in the strong class by (1). Scope/rendering: NCG.SpectralTriple carries no real structure J or grading gamma, so 'equivariantly for J and gamma when marked' / 'symmetric cutoffs preserve the J, gamma signs' are not encoded; spectral projections are finite unions of eigenspaces of D (the symmetric cutoffs 1_[-R,R](D) are special cases); P_n up-arrow I is rendered as P_{n+1}P_n = P_n plus strong convergence. |
| `thm:essential-image` | corollary | Reconstruction classes and their essential images | [`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.essential_image`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.strongReconstruction_essImage`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.isStrongCompression_of_spectralProjectionSequence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.range_diracSpectralScreen_eq_spectralSubspace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.normReconstruction_essImage_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.normReconstruction_essImage_iff_spectrallyQuasidiagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.spectralCommutatorDecay_iff_spectrallyQuasidiagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.isNormMultiplicativeCompression_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.sqdProperty_le_essImage_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.toeplitzObject_not_essImage_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.toeplitzObject_not_spectrallyQuasidiagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EssentialImageReconstruction.toeplitzObject_not_isSpectrallyQuasidiagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EssentialImageReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.finiteReconstruction_essImage`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.exists_separating_coordinates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedCommutantCoordinates.MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple.finite_calibrated_recovery`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedCommutantCoordinates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedCommutantCoordinates.MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple.finite_calibrated_recovery_sharp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedCommutantCoordinates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SpectralCompressionsSequential.spectral_compressions`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SpectralCompressionsSequential.lean) | All three lines proved with the categories and functors of ReconstructionCategories.lean (def:reconstruction-categories), now that thm:spectral-compressions is proved. essential_image bundles: (1) EssIm(S_fin^cal) = Spec_fin (finiteReconstruction_essImage; recovery from commutator data plus q_S coordinates: finite_calibrated_recovery/_sharp). (2) EssIm(S_cpt^strong,cal) = Spec_cpt^sep: every separable object has a SpectralProjectionSequence (finite resolvent screens of exists_monotone_screens are proved to be spectral projections 1_B(D), range_diracSpectralScreen_eq_spectralSubspace), and every spectral projection sequence satisfies all clauses of eq:strong-compression-topology incl. marks, resolvents, commutator extensions and product defects (isStrongCompression_of_spectralProjectionSequence). (3) X in EssIm(S_cpt^norm,cal) iff some spectral projection sequence has \|\|[P_n,pi(a)]\|\| -> 0 on A_D (normReconstruction_essImage_iff; transport of P_n along unitary equivalences proved), iff SpectralCompressionsSequential.SpectrallyQuasidiagonal X (spectralCommutatorDecay_iff_spectrallyQuasidiagonal: every finite-rank spectral projection 1_B(D) is a finite resolvent screen, via linear independence of eigenvectors). (4) strictness: the Toeplitz triple (A = alg(I,S,S*), number operator, as a CompactMarkedObject for every mark type; also a non-vacuity witness) is in the strong but not the norm essential image and is not sqd in either form. Recorded commutant coordinates of the constructed (C2) sources are the zero functionals (the source structure allows any). Scope/rendering (flag for author): in def:reconstruction-categories the (C2)/(C3) sources use arbitrary increasing finite-rank spectral projections 1_{B_n}(D), while eq:sqd asks for 'symmetric' projections, equivariant for J and gamma; ReconstructionCategories.IsSpectrallyQuasidiagonal encodes this as windows 1_{[-R,R]}(D). Line 3 is proved with sqd in the screen form under which thm:spectral-compressions was closed (SpectralCompressionsSequential.SpectrallyQuasidiagonal: finite spectral screens P_n increasing to I with \|\|[P_n,pi(a)]\|\| -> 0 on A_D, no J/gamma clause - NCG.SpectralTriple carries no J/gamma); the windowed equivariant form is proved to be contained in the norm essential image (sqdProperty_le_essImage_norm), but the reverse inclusion is false in general for the (C3) category as written: e.g. D with simple eigenvalues k and k+3/2 (k in N), A_D = C[T], T the involution swapping the k and k+3/2 eigenvectors, has [D,T] bounded, exact multiplicativity along the block-closed screens {0..n} u {3/2..n+3/2}, yet every window [-R,R] cuts the block floor(R), so \|\|[P,T]\|\| = 1 (informal, not formalized). So the literal paper equality EssIm(norm) = Spec^sqd holds only if 'symmetric' means self-adjoint (the reading of the proved parent) or if (C2)/(C3) are restricted to symmetric windows; the J/gamma-equivariance clause of eq:sqd is not carried by the (C3) sources. [Coordinator 2 Oct 2026] Accepted as proved under the reading 'symmetric spectral projections' = self-adjoint spectral projections 1_B(D) (the reading under which thm:spectral-compressions was proved). Manuscript query: if 'symmetric' means windows 1_{[-R,R]}(D), the norm line fails (informal counterexample: eigenvalues k, k+3/2 with T swapping pairs; block screens are multiplicative but every window cuts a block, \|\|[P,T]\|\| = 1); only the inclusion window-sqd ⊆ norm essential image is proved (sqdProperty_le_essImage_norm). The J/γ-equivariance clause of eq:sqd is vacuous for the encoded triples, which carry no J/γ marks. |
| `thm:supp-graph-square` | theorem | Graph Hodge square and exact Lipschitz seminorm | [`RenewalGeometry.CanonicalGraphSpectralization.canonical_graph_spectralization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CanonicalGraphSpectralizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryGraphSpectralization.stationary_rate_dirac_square`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryGraphSpectralizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryGraphSpectralization.stationary_rate_commutator_energy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryGraphSpectralizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryGraphSpectralization.connected_commutator_kernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryGraphSpectralizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteConnesDistanceMetric.connesDistance_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteConnesDistanceMetricExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteConnesDistanceMetric.connesDistance_triangle`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteConnesDistanceMetricExact.lean) | From positive masses, nonnegative stationary rates and connected conductance support: d^*d f = -(L f + L^dagger f) = -2 L_s f in vertex coordinates, \|\|[D,f]\|\|^2 = max_x (1/m_x) sum_y c_xy \|f(y)-f(x)\|^2 for real f (stationary_rate_commutator_energy, stated as the sup norm of x -> (1/m_x) sum_y c_xy \|f y - f x\|^2), the kernel is exactly the constants, and the resulting Connes distance on V is a metric. The added carre-du-champ normalization L(f)^2 = 2\|\|Gamma_s(f)\|\|_inf with Gamma_s(f)(x) = (1/(2m_x)) sum_y c_xy \|f(y)-f(x)\|^2 (eq:supp-carre-du-champ) is the same sup norm rescaled by the positive constant 1/2 (norm_smul); it is not restated as a separate Lean lemma. Scoped: real-valued vertex functions. |
| `cth:supp-extensive-kernel` | proposition | — | [`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowth.extensive_universal_oneform_kernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowthExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowth.connected_graph_kernel_dimension`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowthExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowth.periodic_A3_kernel_dimension`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowthExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowth.universalDiracKernelEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormDiracKernelGrowthExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.retained_universal_oneform_zero_modes_no_compact_resolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.not_finiteDimensional_partialKer_of_injections`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.not_hasCompactResolvent_of_injections`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.finiteDimensional_partialKer_of_hasCompactResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.partialKer_le_eigenspace_resolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.HasCompactResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.IsResolventAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolvent.partialKer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/UniversalOneFormKernelNoCompactResolventExact.lean) | Proved. Kernel formula dim ker D = 2e+2-n (natural-number spelling of 2e-n+2) for the block Dirac (v,w) -> (d^dagger w, d v) with dim V = n, dim W = 2e (both orientations retained), connectedness rendered as dim ker d = 1 (connected_graph_kernel_dimension); periodic A3 count 11n+2 for e = 6n (periodic_A3_kernel_dimension). Consequence (new): the limit operator is an arbitrary, possibly unbounded, partially defined operator T : H ->l.[K] H (LinearPMap) on a Hilbert space over K = R or C (RCLike); its kernel is partialKer T; compact resolvent = some bounded two-sided inverse R of T - z (IsResolventAt: range in the domain, right and left inverse) is a compact operator (HasCompactResolvent). 'Compatible cofinal limit retaining all zero modes' = a cofinal family of connected periodic A3 stages (dim V_k = n_k, dim W_k = 12 n_k, dim ker d_k = 1, n_k unbounded) with real-linear injections ker D_k -> ker T. retained_universal_oneform_zero_modes_no_compact_resolvent proves dim ker D_k = 11 n_k + 2, ker T not finite-dimensional, and no compact resolvent. Key step: ker T lies in the eigenspace of R(z) at the nonzero eigenvalue (-z)^{-1} (and is trivial if z = 0), which is finite-dimensional for compact R (Mathlib ContinuousLinearMap.finite_dimensional_eigenspace). Compatibility maps between stages and self-adjointness are not needed and not assumed (the Lean result is stronger). The old arithmetic stand-in periodic_A3_nullity_unbounded is no longer cited. Vacuity: hypotheses jointly satisfiable (e.g. T = 0 on an infinite-dimensional Hilbert space with any injections of the finite stage kernels); no Lean witness added. Standard axioms only. |

| `prop:A3-covariance` | proposition | Jump covariance and the independent metric | [`RenewalGeometry.relational_flat_vacuum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalFlatVacuum.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.a3Roots`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalFlatVacuum.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FiniteDifferenceConsistency.tight_frame_energy`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3FiniteDifferenceConsistencyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatTorusMetric.flatDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3FlatTorusMetricExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatTorusMetric.flatDistance_eq_infDist`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3FlatTorusMetricExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatTorusMetric.lattice_isClosed`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3FlatTorusMetricExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicQuotientDistance.distance_periodic_right`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicQuotientDistanceExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicQuotientDistance.distance_comm`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PeriodicQuotientDistanceExact.lean) | Proved, with the scoping of thm:supp-A3-distance. The frame identity sum_alpha alpha alpha^T = 8 I for the twelve A3 roots in R^3 coordinates of W_0 is relational_flat_vacuum (energy form tight_frame_energy); with k_{alpha,h} = 1/(8h^2) the covariance Q_h = sum_alpha k_{alpha,h} (h alpha)(h alpha)^T = (1/8) sum_alpha alpha alpha^T = I is this identity times the scalar h^2/(8h^2) = 1/8 (arithmetic, not a separate lemma), hence independent of h. The distance d_g is flatDistance = infDist(x - y, Lambda) (flatDistance_eq_infDist), independent of lifts (distance_periodic_right, distance_comm); the infimum is attained (min) because the lattice is closed in a proper space (lattice_isClosed with Mathlib's IsClosed.exists_infDist_eq_dist; not stated as a separate lemma). Scoped: as in thm:supp-A3-distance, d_g is taken as the flat quotient metric directly; its identification with the Riemannian geodesic distance of g = Q_h^{-1} is not formalised (no Riemannian structure in Lean). |
| `thm:supp-A3-finite` | theorem | $A_3$ Hodge identities and smooth-function consistency | | Proved. All four clauses on the actual finite periodic A3 graph (vertices V_h = h Lambda/Lambda as Fin 3 -> ZMod d, mesh h = 1/d, rates 1/(8h^2), masses h^3, root steps reduced modulo the period): (i) eq:supp-A3-Lip, L_h(f)^2 = max_x (1/(8h^2)) sum_alpha \|f(x+h alpha)-f(x)\|^2 (graphLipschitz_sample_sq_eq, previous pass); (ii) eq:supp-A3-square via the general stationary theorem (previous pass); (iii) NEW eq:supp-A3-Lip-convergence with the explicit constant C_{A3} = 4 + sum_i \|b_i\| = 4 + 3 sqrt 2: graphLipschitz_rate shows \|L_h(F\|V_h) - sup_M \|grad F\|\| <= (4 + sum_i \|b_i\|) M_2 h for every lattice-periodic C^1 field F whose gradient is M_2-Lipschitz (sup_M \|grad F\| rendered as sSup of the range of \|grad F\|; since grad F is periodic this is the L^infinity norm on the torus). Ingredients: first-order Taylor remainder along each root via the mean-value inequality (rootDifference_error_le, \|(F(x+h alpha)-F(x))/h - alpha.grad F\| <= \|alpha\|^2 M_2 h = 2 M_2 h), the existing reverse-triangle/tight-frame estimate (sqrt_energy_error_le) giving a pointwise error 4 M_2 h, a mesh-covering lemma (exists_vertex_near: every point of the torus is within h sum_i \|b_i\| of a vertex after a lattice translation, via floors in the period basis and ZMod.val_intCast) and periodicity of the gradient (gradient_periodic) to compare the max over the mesh with the sup over the torus; (iv) NEW generator clause: tendstoUniformly_generator proves (L_h F)(x) := (1/(8h^2)) sum_alpha (F(x+h alpha)-F(x)) -> (1/2) Delta F uniformly on the torus as h -> 0+ for every lattice-periodic C^2 field (F, DF, D^2F with D^2F continuous; the Hessian is uniformly continuous by periodicity), with Delta F = sum_i D^2F(e_i,e_i); ingredients: uniform second-order Taylor remainder by two one-dimensional mean-value inequalities (second_order_remainder), cancellation of the linear terms (sum of the twelve roots is zero) and the polarized tight-frame identity sum_alpha B(alpha,alpha) = 8 sum_i B(e_i,e_i) for every bilinear form (sum_root_bilinear, lem:supp-A3-frame), giving (h^2/2) 8 Delta F /(8 h^2) = Delta F/2; generator_sample_eq identifies the sampled generator at the vertices with the finite-graph generator of eq:supp-A3-square applied to F\|V_h. Renderings disclosed: the paper's C^2 norm enters the rate only through the Lipschitz constant of grad F, so the rate theorem takes that constant M_2 (<= \|F\|_{C^2}) as hypothesis; the generator limit is proved under C^2 (weaker than the paper's C^4) as uniform convergence without a rate, exactly as the theorem states (the O(h^2 \|F\|_{C^4}) rate mentioned only in the proof is not formalised). |

_steps`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.card_root_steps_le_three`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.interp_point`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.interp_meshVertex`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.interp_eq_barycentric`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.exists_affine_eq_interp_on_meshSimplex`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.abs_interp_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.norm_fderiv_interp_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.cell_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.volume_cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.norm_sub_le_of_mem_cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.exists_add_mem_cell_index`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.index_eq_of_mem_cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.index_periodic`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.lift_eq_of_mem_cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.abs_lift_sub_interp_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.kuhnExt`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.kuhnSimplex`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.mem_kuhnSimplex_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.exists_mem_kuhnSimplex`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.mem_convexHull_common_vertices`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.kuhnExt_eq_barycentric`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.abs_kuhnExt_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.abs_kuhnExt_sub_floor_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.KuhnLovaszExtension.kuhnVertex_eq_add_steps`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/KuhnLovaszExtensionExact.lean) | Proved, in the library's Euclidean realization Space = R^3 of W_0 (period basis b_i = three roots, coordinates = T^{-1}, h = 1/d). New infrastructure KuhnLovaszExtensionExact: the Freudenthal-Kuhn (Lovasz) extension kuhnExt g u = int_0^1 g(floor(u + s 1)) ds of lattice data, proved to be the continuous piecewise-affine (P1) interpolant on the Kuhn simplices k + K_sigma, K_sigma = conv{0, e_s1, e_s1+e_s2, 1} (kuhnExt_eq_barycentric; mem_kuhnSimplex_iff identifies k + K_sigma with 1 >= t_s1 >= t_s2 >= t_s3 >= 0), with unit-step Lipschitz and floor-comparison estimates. Mesh (A3KuhnInterpolationExact, meshSimplex d k sigma = hT(k + K_sigma)): covering (exists_mem_meshSimplex), face-to-face intersection = convex hull of common vertices (mem_convexHull_common_meshVertices), invariance under the period lattice k -> k + d z (add_mem_meshSimplex_iff), shape regularity with h-independent constants (contains the ball of radius h/8 about the barycentre, diameter <= 6h), descent to M: vertices project onto V_h and every point of V_h is a vertex (meshVertex_sub_point_vertex_mem_lattice, point_eq_meshVertex), vertices of one simplex distinct in V_h and projection injective on each simplex for d >= 2, and for d >= 3 a simplex meets another in at most one lattice translate (lattice_translate_unique), so projected simplices intersect in single common faces; every mesh edge joins vertices differing by at most three root steps h*alpha (meshVertex_sub_eq_sum_root_steps, card_root_steps_le_three). Interpolant I_h f = interp d f: agrees with f on V_h, continuous, periodic, affine on every mesh simplex, \|I_h f(q) - I_h f(p)\| <= sqrt 24 L_h(f) \|q - p\| and \|\|D I_h f\|\| <= sqrt 24 L_h(f) everywhere (eq:A3-interpolation-bound; L_h(f) = graphLipschitz (mass d) (conductance d) f, via the single-root bound sqrt 8 h L_h and sum_i (T^{-1}w)_i^2 <= \|w\|^2). Cells Q_{h,x} = x + hT[0,1)^3 (cell d x): cell_eq (= point d x + h * fundamental cell), volume_cell (volume = h^3 times the volume of the fundamental cell T[0,1)^3, i.e. normalized Haar measure h^3), diameter <= 6h, partition of the torus (exists_add_mem_cell_index, index_eq_of_mem_cell, index_periodic). eq:A3-cell-interpolation-bound with C = 3 sqrt 8: \|f-hat_h - I_h f\| <= 3 sqrt 8 h L_h(f) everywhere, f-hat_h = A3PeriodicStepFunctionLift.lift (= f(x) on Q_{h,x}, lift_eq_of_mem_cell). Rendering notes: the triangulation property is stated as covering + face-to-face (intersection = conv of common vertices); the quotient simplicial structure is stated through lattice invariance, vertex projection, injectivity on simplices and uniqueness of the meeting translate (d >= 3) rather than through a separate simplicial-complex API on M. Standard axioms only. |
| `lem:supp-A3-compactness` | lemma | — | [`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolantCompactness.a3_interpolant_compactness`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/A3KuhnInterpolantCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolantCompactness.lipschitzWith_interp_of_le_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/A3KuhnInterpolantCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolantCompactness.exists_parallelepiped_add_lattice`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/A3KuhnInterpolantCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolantCompactness.flatDistance_add_lattice`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/A3KuhnInterpolantCompactnessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.interp`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.abs_interp_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.interp_periodic`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3KuhnInterpolation.interp_point`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3KuhnInterpolationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SharpLipschitzLimit.abs_sub_le_connesDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/A3SharpLipschitzLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SharpLipschitzLimit.tendsto_point_approxVertex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/A3SharpLipschitzLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SharpLipschitzLimit.tendsto_flatDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/A3SharpLipschitzLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3ConnesFlatUniformConvergence.tendsto_distanceError_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3ConnesFlatUniformConvergenceExact.lean) | Proved (a3_interpolant_compactness). Mesh sizes h_n = 1/(m_n + 1) with m_n -> infinity (any admissible h_n = 1/d_n decreasing to 0, possibly along a subsequence of meshes); f_n : V_{h_n} -> R with L_{h_n}(f_n) = graphLipschitz <= 1 and f_n vanishing at some vertex (the paper's x_{0,n} -> x_0 is not needed, so the Lean hypothesis is weaker). Conclusion: a subsequence of the Kuhn piecewise-affine interpolants I_{h_n} f_n of lem:A3-periodic-triangulation (A3KuhnInterpolation.interp) converges uniformly on all of Space to a lattice-periodic F (a function on M) with \|F z - F z'\| <= d_g(z, z'); hence F is 1-Lipschitz (rendering of F in W^{1,infinity}(M): a periodic Lipschitz function), \|\|grad F\|\| <= 1 at every point, and F is differentiable with \|\|grad F\|\| <= 1 almost everywhere (eq:supp-A3-limit-Lip, via Rademacher). Proof: the interpolants are periodic and uniformly sqrt 24-Lipschitz (eq:A3-interpolation-bound) and anchored, so Arzela-Ascoli on the ball of radius 6 (containing a fundamental domain) plus periodicity gives uniform convergence on Space; the sharp constant comes from \|f_n x - f_n y\| <= d_n(x, y) (finite Connes distance) and the proved uniform Connes-to-flat convergence tendsto_distanceError_zero (thm:supp-A3-distance is proved in the ledger, cited as an unconditional theorem, not as a hypothesis), instead of the paper's weak-* / summation-by-parts argument. Non-vacuity example (zero functions on the full mesh sequence) included. Standard axioms only. |
| `lem:A3-uniform-distance-smoothing` | lemma | Uniform smoothing of the torus distance | [`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRate.a3_uniform_distance_smoothing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRate.smoothedDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRate.scaledBump`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRate.norm_fderiv_fderiv_smoothedDistance_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRate.norm_fderiv_smoothedDistance_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRate.integral_norm_fderiv_scaledBump_normed`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRate.scaledBump_normed_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LipschitzConvolutionDerivativeModulus.norm_fderiv_convolution_sub_le_of_lipschitz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/LipschitzConvolutionDerivativeModulusExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistance.contDiff_smoothDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistance.smoothDistance_periodic`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistance.abs_smoothDistance_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SmoothedFlatDistance.norm_fderiv_smoothDistance_le_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3SmoothedFlatDistanceExact.lean) | Proved (a3_uniform_distance_smoothing). eps_0 = 1, C_M = 8 + int \|\|D eta_1\|\|. For every centre y (a lift in the Euclidean realization Space of W_0) and 0 < eps < 1, F_{y,eps} = eta_eps * d_g(y, .) with eta_eps the normalized Mathlib bump of radii (eps/2, eps) is C^infinity, lattice periodic (descends to M), \|F - d_g(y, .)\| <= eps, \|\|DF\|\| <= 1, and \|F(p)\| + \|\|DF(p)\|\| + \|\|D^2 F(p)\|\| <= C_M eps^{-1} for every p (the C^2(M) norm rendered as the sup of \|F\| + \|\|DF\|\| + \|\|D^2F\|\| with D^2F = fderiv (fderiv F)). The eps^{-1} rate: new general lemma norm_fderiv_convolution_sub_le_of_lipschitz (D(k*f) is K int\|\|Dk\|\|-Lipschitz for compactly supported C^1 scalar k and K-Lipschitz f; one derivative on the kernel), and the exact scaling int\|\|D eta_eps\|\| = eps^{-1} int\|\|D eta_1\|\| (bumps with fixed radius ratio are rescalings, eta_eps(x) = eps^{-3} eta_1(x/eps), Haar change of variables). Rendering: d_g(y, .) instead of d_g(., y) (the distance is symmetric). Standard axioms only. |
| `thm:supp-A3-distance` | theorem | Connes-distance convergence on the periodic $A_3$ lattice | [`RenewalGeometry.A3ConnesFlatUniformConvergence.tendsto_distanceError_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3ConnesFlatUniformConvergenceExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3ConnesFlatUniformConvergence.eventually_uniform_connes_flat_error`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3ConnesFlatUniformConvergenceExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatTorusMetric.flatDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3FlatTorusMetricExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3FlatTorusMetric.flatDistance_eq_infDist`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/A3FlatTorusMetricExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.A3SpectralizationRate.sum_root_mul`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/A3SpectralizationRateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteConnesDistanceAttainment.connesDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteConnesDistanceAttainmentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CanonicalGraphSpectralization.canonical_graph_spectralization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CanonicalGraphSpectralizationExact.lean) | sup over vertex pairs of \|d_h(x,y) - d_g(x,y)\| -> 0 is proved with d_h the actual finite commutator-supremum Connes distance of the A3 graph triple and d_g = flatDistance = infDist(x - y, Lambda_A3) = min over lattice vectors of \|\|x - y + l\|\| (flatDistance_eq_infDist), exactly eq:A3-independent-distance of prop:A3-covariance. The revised identification of d_g as 'the flat geodesic distance of the metric determined by the jump covariance' is matched by sum_root_mul (sum_alpha alpha_i alpha_j = 8 delta_ij in the Lean coordinates, i.e. Q_h = (1/8) sum alpha (x) alpha = I, so g = Q_h^{-1} is the Euclidean inner product used by flatDistance). Scoped: the covariance identity and the convergence theorem are separate Lean statements linked through the common Space inner product; mesh index d = n + 1. |
| `thm:supp-graph-realization` | theorem | Stationary Markov realization fibre of a metric graph triple | [`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.stationary_markov_realization_fibre`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.IsRealization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.rates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.reversibleRates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.isRealization_reversibleRates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.isRealization_rates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.rates_pos_of_mem`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.sum_pairFlux_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.IsRealization.existsUnique_current`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.IsRealization.currentOf_mem`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.IsRealization.currentOf_mem_closed`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.dirac_rates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.closedRealizationEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.realizationEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.IsRealization.toStationaryRenewalGraph`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.isRealization_of_stationaryRenewalGraph`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.abs_lt_conductance_iff_flux_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CanonicalGraphSpectralization.canonical_graph_spectralization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CanonicalGraphSpectralizationExact.lean) | Revised to the closed fibre; every clause is covered. (R1) isRealization_reversibleRates: c/m is a stationary realization with detailed balance. (R2) isRealization_rates: every j in the CLOSED polytope Jbar(G,c) gives the stationary realization k = (c+j)/m with nonnegative rates (stationarity from sum_pairFlux_sub: sum_y (q_xy - q_yx) = 2 (B j)_x); 'currents in J(G,c) are exactly those with positive flux in both directions' is rates_pos_of_mem (=>) and IsRealization.currentOf_mem (<=), packaged as realizationEquiv. (R3) IsRealization.existsUnique_current: every stationary realization with masses m and symmetrized flux c (IsRealization: zero diagonal, nonnegative rates, mass stationarity, (q+q^T)/2 = c on edges and 0 off edges) is uniquely (c+j)/m with j in Jbar(G,c) (currentOf_mem_closed). (R4) dirac_rates: the Hodge-Dirac matrix of every current realization is dirac m c. eq:supp-realization-fibre RReal_graph = Jbar(G,c) is the explicit bijection closedRealizationEquiv : Jbar(G,c) ≃ {stationary realizations}, and J(G,c) ≃ {positive-edge realizations} is realizationEquiv. stationary_markov_realization_fibre bundles (R1), the open-polytope part of (R2), (R3), (R4) and realizationEquiv; the closed-polytope (R2) and closedRealizationEquiv are the separate cited lemmas. IsRealization.toStationaryRenewalGraph / isRealization_of_stationaryRenewalGraph tie realizations to def:supp-renewal-graph. Scoped: simple graph (the paper's 'up to unobserved parallel-channel refinements'), diagonal of k normalized to 0; 'strictly positive relative interior' is the strict-inequality set. |
| `cor:supp-fibre-dimension` | corollary | — | [`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.fibre_affine_dimension_and_faithfulness`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.closedCurrentPolytope_affineDim`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.currentPolytope_affineDim`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.vectorSpan_closedCurrentPolytope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.vectorSpan_currentPolytope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.realCycleSpace_finrank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.reversible_unique_iff_tree`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.realCycleSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphFibreAffineDimension.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.spectralization_not_faithful`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.closedRealizationEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean) | Revised statement proved for a connected simple graph G presented by an orientation (SimpleOrientation, as in def:supp-current-polytope), positive masses m and positive edge conductances c. fibre_affine_dimension_and_faithfulness bundles: (1) the closed polytope Jbar(G,c) and its relative interior J(G,c) both have affine dimension \|E\|+1-\|V\| = b_1(G), stated as finrank_R of the direction of their affine spans (closedCurrentPolytope_affineDim, currentPolytope_affineDim): both vector spans equal the REAL cycle space ker B (vectorSpan_*: every cycle current has a positive multiple in the open box around 0) and finrank_R ker B = \|E\|+1-\|V\| (realCycleSpace_finrank, rank-nullity for B and B^T with ker B^T = constants by connectedness); (2) (forall stationary realizations k of (m,G,c), k = reversibleRates m c) <-> \|E\|+1 = \|V\| (reversible_unique_iff_tree; tree = connected with \|E\| = \|V\|-1, transferred to realizations via existsUnique_current / rates_injective); (3) if \|V\| < \|E\|+1 (b_1 > 0) two distinct stationary realizations in J(G,c) share the Hodge-Dirac matrix (spectralization_not_faithful). Natural-number subtraction \|E\|+1-\|V\| is exact because \|V\| <= \|E\|+1 is proved for connected G (card_le_card_edges_add_one). Non-vacuity example: the one-vertex tree. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `prop:supp-entropy` | proposition | — | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.entropyProduction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.entropyEdge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.entropyEdge_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.entropyEdge_pos_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.entropyProduction_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean) | The revision restricts the statement to j in J(G,c), which is exactly the Lean hypothesis: sigma(j) = 2 sum_e j_e log((c_e+j_e)/(c_e-j_e)) (entropyProduction, entropyEdge) is nonnegative edgewise (entropyEdge_nonneg) and vanishes exactly at j = 0 (entropyProduction_eq_zero_iff) under 0 < c_e and \|j_e\| < c_e (the Lean does not even need B j = 0). Scoped: the formula is taken as the definition (its derivation from (1/2) sum (q_xy - q_yx) log(q_xy/q_yx) is not compiled); the added boundary sentence (extended value +infinity on Jbar(G,c) minus J(G,c) when a directional flux vanishes) is an interpretive convention that is not encoded - the real-valued Lean formula is only meaningful on the open polytope. |
| `lem:supp-zero-affinity` | lemma | — | [`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.zero_affinity_class_forces_reversibility`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.affinity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.CurrentFibre`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.IsExact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean) | Proved in the paper's generality: for a real oriented incidence matrix B : Matrix V E ℝ (finite V, E) and conductances c, a current j in the fibre (B j = 0 and \|j_e\| < c_e) whose affinity a_e = Real.artanh (j_e / c_e) is exact (a = Bᵀ φ for some vertex potential φ, i.e. [a] = 0 in ℝ^E / Ran Bᵀ) is zero. Proof pairs φ against B j to get Σ_e a_e j_e = 0, each term nonnegative and vanishing only at j_e = 0 (Real.artanh_nonneg/nonpos/eq_zero_iff). Connectedness is not needed for this conclusion (a = Bᵀφ = 0 already forces j = 0 edgewise). |

>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.contDiffAt_artanh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeDiffeomorphism.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.strictConvexOn_affinityFunctional`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeDiffeomorphism.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.strictConvexOn_logCosh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeDiffeomorphism.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.meanZeroMinimizer_unique_of_strictConvexOn`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeDiffeomorphism.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.isConnectedIncidence_twoVertex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeDiffeomorphism.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.nonlinear_hodge`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.affinityClass_bijOn`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.existsUnique_current_of_class`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.exists_meanZeroMinimizer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.tendsto_regularizedFunctional_cocompact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.affinityFunctional`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.currentOfPotential`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.isConnectedIncidence_incidenceReal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NonlinearAffinityHodge.existsUnique_current_of_connected`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/NonlinearAffinityHodgeExact.lean) | Proved. For a real oriented incidence B with B^T 1 = 0 and ker B^T = constants (IsConnectedIncidence; derived from graph connectedness of a simple orientation by isConnectedIncidence_incidenceReal) and c > 0: (diffeomorphism) affinity_diffeomorphism / affinityPartialHomeomorph: the affinity map j -> [artanh(j/c)] is an OpenPartialHomeomorph from the cycle space Ker B (finite-dim normed subtype) with source exactly the open fibre J(G,c) = {j in Ker B : \|j\|<c} and target all of H^1 = R^E / Ran B^T (quotient norm; Ran B^T closed), C^infinity on the source (artanh smooth on (-1,1): hasDerivAt_artanh, contDiffAt_artanh) with C^infinity inverse (contDiff_classCycle); its differential v -> [diag(1/(c(1-(j/c)^2))) v] is a linear isomorphism Ker B ~= H^1 (affinityClassDerivEquiv: injective by the positive weighted pairing sum w_e v_e^2 = <Bv,phi> = 0, and dim Ker B = dim H^1 by rank-nullity + rank B^T = rank B, finrank_cycles_eq_finrank_firstCohomology). Smoothness of the inverse is by Mathlib's C^n inverse function theorem (ContDiffAt.to_localInverse) at every point, globalised by bijectivity via HasStrictFDerivAt.localInverse_unique (the paper uses the implicit function theorem on the Euler map; same content). (strict convexity) strictConvexOn_affinityFunctional: F_alpha is StrictConvexOn the mean-zero potentials (log cosh strictly convex since tanh strictly increasing; B^T injective on mean-zero potentials); uniqueness of the minimiser follows from it (meanZeroMinimizer_unique_of_strictConvexOn). (coercivity, minimiser, inverse formula) tendsto_regularizedFunctional_cocompact (F_alpha + (sum phi)^2 -> infinity; equals F_alpha on V_0), unique mean-zero minimiser phi_alpha, a_alpha = a0 + B^T phi_alpha, j_alpha = c tanh a_alpha with B j_alpha = 0 is the inverse image 𝔄^{-1}([a0]) and the unique current of class alpha (classCurrent_mk_eq_currentOfPotential, nonlinear_hodge_diffeomorphism). Non-vacuity: isConnectedIncidence_twoVertex (two vertices, one edge) and an example instantiating contDiff_classCurrent. |
| `cor:supp-faithful-modular` | corollary | — | [`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.current_eq_of_affinity_class_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.faithful_modular_enrichment`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean) | Re-verified against the new statement (editorial change): the enriched object is renamed S_aff(j) (graph triple plus affinity class) and the conclusion 'faithful on each finite graph fibre' is restated as 'the affinity class determines the directed generator throughout the strictly positive fibre' J(G,c) = {j in Ker B : \|j_e\| < c_e}, which is exactly the Lean CurrentFibre B c hypothesis of both cited theorems. Proved directly, bypassing thm:supp-nonlinear-Hodge: two currents in the fibre 𝒥(G,c) whose affinities differ by an exact cochain (same class [a] in H^1 = ℝ^E / Ran Bᵀ) coincide (strict monotonicity of artanh via Real.artanh_le_artanh_iff / artanh_injOn); hence for fixed (G,m,c) the class [a] determines the directed fluxes q = c ± j (forwardFlux/reverseFlux) and the stationary rates k_xy = q_xy/m_x (forwardRate/reverseRate of FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre, eq. supp-current-realization). The metric packet (C(V),H_{m,c},D_{m,c},J,γ) depends on (G,m,c) only, so this is the faithfulness claim. The explicit inverse a_α (minimiser of the convex functional of thm:supp-nonlinear-Hodge) is not constructed here; only the injectivity the corollary asserts. |
| `thm:supp-current-chain` | theorem | Graph quotients and entropy contraction | [`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.incidence_mul_edgeMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.edgeSum_mem_cycleSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.edgeSum_mem_currentPolytope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.edgeSum_cycleSpace_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.range_cycleMap_eq_top`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.finrank_ker_cycleMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.finrank_ker_cycleMap_eq_betti`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.cycleSpace_finrank_of_connected`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.entropyProduction_edgeSum_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.current_chain_functor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean) | Proved over R for finite oriented graphs (tail/head maps, multi-edges allowed) and a vertex quotient q : V -> V-bar with induced coarse graph in which every fine edge is either mapped to a coarse edge with the quotient endpoints or is internal to a fibre and dropped (VertexQuotient); Q_V, Q_E are the fibre-summation matrices. (i) chain identity d-bar Q_E = Q_V d as a matrix identity, hence Q_E Z(G) <= Z(G-bar); (ii) Q_E Curr(G,c) <= Curr(G-bar, c-bar) with c-bar = Q_E c, under the hypothesis that every coarse edge has a nonempty fibre ('retains every inter-fibre edge class', needed for the strict inequality); (iii) with connected fibres (through internal edges) and retained classes, Q_E : Z(G) -> Z(G-bar) is onto (constructive lift: one representative per coarse edge, divergence completed by internal path flows), so the short sequence is exact and dim ker = dim Z(G) - dim Z(G-bar); with G and G-bar connected and nonempty this is (\|E\|-\|V\|+1) - (\|E-bar\|-\|V-bar\|+1) via a real Betti-number lemma; (iv) sigma_{G-bar}(Q_E j) <= sigma_G(j) for j in the open polytope with c > 0, via the log-sum inequality (sum_rateDivergence_ge_total) applied to forward/reverse fluxes c +- j on each coarse-edge fibre. Scoped hypotheses disclosed: finiteness, Nonempty V / V-bar and connectedness of both graphs for the Betti form of the kernel dimension. |
