try.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshSequence.exists_error_tendsto_zero_of_approx_of_compactSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphApproxChains.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshSequence.tendsto_edgeLipschitz_of_approx`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphApproxChains.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshSequence.global_metric_of_approx`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphApproxChains.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshGraph`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphRatioSlack.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshSequence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphRatioSlack.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshGraph.ofExact`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphRatioSlack.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshSequence.global_metric`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphRatioSlack.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EndpointBlockMetricHead.norm_commutator_eq_edgeLipschitz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EndpointBlockMetricHeadExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EndpointBlockMetricHead.edgeLipschitz_eq_sup'`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EndpointBlockMetricHeadExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EndpointBlockMetricHead.edgeConnesDistance_eq_graphDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EndpointBlockMetricHeadExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EndpointBlockMetricHead.sSup_commutator_unit_ball_eq_graphDistance`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/EndpointBlockMetricHeadExact.lean) | Proved on genuine closed Riemannian manifolds in Mathlib's sense (Mathlib.Geometry.Manifold.Riemannian). Main theorem MeshGraphRiemannian.lean: ApproxMeshSequence.global_metric_riemannian: for M a compact manifold modelled on a boundaryless model I (IsManifold I 1 M), with a continuous Riemannian metric (RiemannianBundle + IsContinuousRiemannianBundle) and its Riemannian distance (IsRiemannianManifold I M: edist = riemannianEDist = inf of lengths of C^1 paths; distance finite, i.e. MetricSpace M), and for EVERY refining sequence of approximate meshes under the paper's general mesh assumptions (ApproxMeshSequence: h-nets V_h, h/rho -> 0, rho -> 0, every pair within rho_h joined, positive lengths with \|l_h/d_g - 1\| <= eps_h -> 0), all four clauses hold: (1) \|\|[D_h^met, pi_h(g)]\|\| = max_e \|g(x_e)-g(y_e)\|/l_e (eq:supp-edge-lip); (2) eventually the pure-state Connes distance equals the graph shortest-path metric; (3) an explicit error sequence -> 0 eventually bounds \|d_h^met(x,y) - d_g(x,y)\| for all vertex pairs (eq:supp-global-metric-convergence); (4) for every C^1 f (ContMDiff I 1 f; the paper's smooth f is a special case), L_h^met(f\|V_h) -> sup_p \|\|df_p\|\|, and ApproxMeshSequence.tendsto_edgeLipschitz_gradient gives the limit as sup_p \|\|grad_g f(p)\|\|_g with the Riesz gradient (norm_gradient: \|\|grad_g f(p)\|\| = \|\|df_p\|\|, inner_gradient: g(grad f, v) = df(v)). global_metric_riemannian_of_connectedSpace states the same for Mathlib's standard setting EMetricSpace M + ConnectedSpace M (finiteness of the distance is derived: edist_ne_top_of_connectedSpace, and EMetricSpace.toMetricSpace keeps the same edist definitionally, toMetricSpace_toEMetricSpace_of_connectedSpace), so the closed CONNECTED manifold hypothesis is exactly the paper's. Bridge built (new infrastructure): (i) MeshGraphApproxChains.lean: HasChains weakened to the approximate chain property HasApproxChains (steps <= rho + delta, sum <= d + delta), proved from continuous near-geodesic paths (hasApproxChains_of_nearGeodesic, by first hitting times + IVT), and every surrogate result re-proved under it (slack delta -> 0 for fixed mesh); (ii) Analysis/RiemannianDistanceLipschitz.lean: exists_nearGeodesic_path (pathELength additivity + riemannianEDist_le_pathELength), lipschitzWith_of_norm_differential_le (Lipschitz <= sup\|\|df\|\|, FTC along near-minimising C^1 paths), exists_sub_mul_dist_le_abs_sub (optimal constant >= \|\|df_p\|\|: chart line in an almost maximising direction, almost-isometric trivialization eventually_norm_symmL_trivializationAt_comp_self_lt), bddAbove_norm_differential (compactness). Disclosed renderings: the paper's 'rho_h below the injectivity radius' is not needed (dropped); ApproxMeshGraph requires eps_h < 1 for every mesh (the paper only has eps_h -> 0; finitely many meshes are irrelevant to the asymptotic clauses) and allows extra edges; d_g is used only through IsRiemannianManifold. Non-vacuity: Mathlib has no compact Riemannian manifold of positive dimension (its only RiemannianBundle instance is the flat metric of an inner product space), so the full compact packet is instantiated only on the one-point space EuclideanSpace R (Fin 0) (example at the end of MeshGraphRiemannian.lean, with a mesh from exists_approxMeshSequence); each Riemannian ingredient is instantiated non-trivially on the Euclidean line (hasApproxChains_real; examples in RiemannianDistanceLipschitz: f = id is 1-Lipschitz via the derivative bound, and the lower bound produces y != 0, norm_differential_id_real); exists_approxMeshSequence shows the mesh hypotheses are satisfiable on every compact metric space. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. |
| `thm:edge-relative-modular-identities` | theorem | Edge reversal and relative density ratios | [`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.reversalConj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.modularOperator`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.modularConjugation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.sourceMul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.terminalMul`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.reversalConj_comp_self`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.reversalConj_modularOperator_reversalConj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.modularOperator_reversalConj_inverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.modularConjugation_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.reversalConj_sourceMul_star_reversalConj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.modularConjugation_sourceMul_star_modularConjugation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.log_modularRatio_eq_two_mul_affinity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.reversalAffinity_eq_artanh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.modularOperator_eq_id_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.reversalAffinity_bar`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean) | Relabelled successor of the removed proved record thm:supp-Tomita (statement identical; Lean applies). Pointwise on l^2(E^or) = (Eor -> C) with an involutive reversal bar, source s, t = s o bar, positive fluxes q: J_E^2 = I (reversalConj_comp_self); J_E Delta_q J_E = Delta_q^{-1} (explicit multiplier plus two-sided inverse, reversalConj_modularOperator_reversalConj, modularOperator_reversalConj_inverse); S_q^2 = I (modularConjugation_sq); log Delta_q(e) = 2 a_e (log_modularRatio_eq_two_mul_affinity; a_e = artanh(j_e/c_e) by reversalAffinity_eq_artanh); reversible branch Delta_q = I iff q_e = q_ebar (modularOperator_eq_id_iff). Convention change disclosed: the new text sets lambda_E(f) = multiplication by f(t(e)) and rho_E(f) = by f(s(e)) (the old record had them swapped). The Lean lemmas reversalConj_sourceMul_star_reversalConj / modularConjugation_sourceMul_star_modularConjugation are stated for an arbitrary source map s, so the new identities J_E lambda_E(f^*) J_E = rho_E(f), S_q lambda_E(f^*) S_q^{-1} = rho_E(f) are their instances at the source map s o bar (whose terminal map is s by involutivity); this instantiation is not a separate lemma. Antiunitarity of J_E is not stated (not part of the statement). |

ularModularHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.harmonicPart_canonicalRepresentative`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.norm_sq_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.d₂_curvature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.exists_generalizedInverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.spectralGeneralizedInverse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeExact.lean) | Proved for an arbitrary finite real Hilbert complex d0,d1,d2 (finite-dimensional real inner product spaces, d1 d0 = 0, d2 d1 = 0). cellular_modular_hodge_normalized packages: existence of a = d0 phi + h_a + d1* psi with P_{Ran d1*} a = d1* G F_a and the norm identity \|\|a\|\|^2 = \|\|d0 phi\|\|^2 + \|\|h_a\|\|^2 + <F_a, G F_a>; uniqueness of the three orthogonal components; the coexact reconstruction; injectivity of [a] -> (h_a, F_a) modulo Ran d0 and surjectivity onto H^1 (+) Ran d1 with canonical representative a_{h,F} = h + d1* G F; Bianchi d2 F_a = 0 (all from cellular_modular_hodge); and the new sentence on potentials: existence of normalised potentials phi in (Ker d0)^perp, psi in (Ker d1*)^perp (exists_normalized_hodge_decomposition) and their uniqueness (normalized_potentials_unique), with the non-uniqueness of unnormalised potentials whenever Ker d0 or Ker d1* is nonzero (potentials_not_unique_of_ker_ne_bot, coexact_potentials_not_unique_of_ker_ne_bot). Rendering: (d1 d1*)^dagger is modelled by any generalised inverse G with T G T = T (the Moore-Penrose inverse is one; existence via spectralGeneralizedInverse / exists_generalizedInverse). |

eometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.walkRatio`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.walkSum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.chainBoundary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.IsCellBoundary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.d₀`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.d₁`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.d₁_comp_d₀`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.cellAffinity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.ratio_eq_exp_cellAffinity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.CellularModel.circle`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeCycleHolonomy.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicTorusCubical.modular_trichotomy_three_torus`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PeriodicTorusCubicalHarmonicExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicTorusCubical.finrank_harmonicSpace_eq_three`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PeriodicTorusCubicalHarmonicExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PeriodicTorusCubical.harmonicSpace_eq_span`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PeriodicTorusCubicalHarmonicExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CellularModularHodge.modular_trichotomy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CellularModularHodgeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.faceHolonomy_eq_exp_faceCurvature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean) | Proved on the cellular model (cellular_modular_trichotomy): a 2-dimensional cell complex = finite NCG.Multigraph one-skeleton G plus integer face-edge incidences eps : P -> E -> Z with boundary-of-boundary zero (IsCellBoundary, from which d1 d0 = 0 is derived, d1_comp_d0); cochains are EuclideanSpace with counting inner products, d0 phi(e) = phi(t e) - phi(s e), d1 a(p) = sum_e eps_{p,e} a_e; the cellular affinity of positive directed fluxes is a_e = 1/2 log(q_e/q_ebar) (cellAffinity), so the edge-ratio transport is q_e/q_ebar = exp(2 a_e). (M1) F_a != 0 gives a two-cell p with face holonomy prod_e (q_e/q_ebar)^{eps_{p,e}} = exp(2F_a(p)) != 1. (M2) F_a = 0 and h_a != 0: every face holonomy is 1 (local flatness) and there is a closed walk w whose integer chain z = walkChain w is a cycle (chainBoundary z = 0) with cycle holonomy prod_e (q_e/q_ebar)^{z_e} = exp(2 sum_e z_e a_e) != 1 (eq:supp-modular-cycle-holonomy). The key step is the exactness criterion exists_d0_iff_walkSum: a cochain is exact iff its signed sum vanishes on every closed walk (proved without connectedness, integrating from a chosen root in each walk-component), and h_a = 0 for exact a (harmonicPart_d0). (M3) F_a = h_a = 0: a = d0 phi and the positive vertex gauge g = exp(2 phi) gives q_e/q_ebar = g(t e)/g(s e), so every cycle holonomy is 1. The case split is not tautological: flat_holonomy_nontrivial_iff shows that, for flat a, nontrivial cycle holonomy holds exactly when h_a != 0. Non-vacuity: the circle (one vertex, one loop, no faces) with q = 2, qbar = 1 inhabits branch (M2). The three-torus clause (global flat sector of dimension three) is finrank_harmonicSpace_eq_three on the periodic cubical complex (Z/n)^3 (earlier pass). Parent thm:supp-modular-Hodge and thm:supp-face-holonomy are proved in the ledger. |
| `thm:supp-face-holonomy` | theorem | — | [`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.faceHolonomy_eq_exp_faceCurvature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.faceHolonomy_eq_one_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.forall_faceHolonomy_eq_one_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.faceCurvature`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ModularAffinityReversalPacket.faceHolonomy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ModularAffinityReversalPacketExact.lean) | Proved: for signed face-incidence coefficients ε_{p,e} ∈ ℤ (the cellular coboundary d_1, so F_a(p) = Σ_e ε_{p,e} a_e is the definition of faceCurvature), Π_e (q_e/q_{ē})^{ε_{p,e}} = exp(2 F_a(p)) with a_e = ½ log(q_e/q_{ē}) and 0 < q_e; descent criterion: all face holonomies equal 1 iff all face-curvature residuals vanish. The same lemma gives eq:supp-modular-cycle-holonomy with ε the oriented-cycle indicator. F_a is encoded through its coboundary formula rather than an abstract Hilbert-complex d_1. |

cessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PhysicalChoiTomographyFrame`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.causalCombPrefixes_unique_from_terminal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.krausMap_reducedKraus`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.SequentialIsometricComb.combAmp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.SequentialIsometricComb.trace_realizedChoi_mul_combProd_choi`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.SequentialIsometricComb.typedBranches`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.typedBranches_op_covariant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.hsBlockUnitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.rep_eq_hsBlockUnitary_conj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.causalReadout`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.causalReadout_hsBlockUnitary_conj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SequentialComb.typedBranches_realization_independent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/CombProcessHistoryRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalGrandTensor.histAlgebra_realization_independent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteOperationalGrandTensor.lean) | Proved (repair: the 'exactly when' clause is no longer true by definition; sequential isometries, the coherent realization and eq:ncg-minimal-unitary-covariance are now constructed/proved). Tomography: for spanning physical slot frames (PhysicalChoiTomographyFrame with span = top) the dual-frame tensor combFrameTensor F p represents ALL multilinear intervention probabilities (combPairing_combFrameTensor: Tr(R (M_N x ... x M_1)) = p(M_1,...,M_N) for every tuple, via the frame-expansion representation lemma combPairing_combDualTensor), the representing tensor is unique (combPairing_injective, combPairing_eq_iff), probabilities on physical trace-normalized positive probes already determine it (multilinear_eq_of_physical, combTensor_eq_of_physical), frame independence is DERIVED rather than assumed (combFrameTensor_frame_independent: both frames represent the same functional), and the tensor is Hermitian when the probabilities are real (combFrameTensor_isHermitian). Process notion defined independently: SequentialIsometricComb (memories A_k, A_0 a point, link maps V_k : H_in (x) A_k -> H_out (x) A_{k+1} with V_k^H V_k = 1); its Choi tensors realizedChoi k = W_k W_k^H with W_k the sequential link (cumulative) - i.e. link followed by final memory discard. 'Deterministic finite quantum process exactly when' is the non-tautological equivalence exists_sequentialIsometricComb_iff / exists_realizing_comb_iff_terminal / exists_sequentialIsometricComb_iff_conditions: necessity (realizedChoi_isDeterministic) is computed from the isometries; sufficiency constructs the links V_k by Gram matching of consecutive minimal rank factors (exists_rank_factor, exists_isometry_of_gram_eq, exists_canonicalLink, canonicalComb); the paper's literal conditions (R^(N) psd, Tr_out R^(k+1) = I (x) R^(k), R^(0) = 1) force prefix positivity (isDeterministicCombThrough_iff_terminal). Support-minimal branch: canonicalComb realizes every R^(k) (canonicalComb_realizedChoi) with card A_k = rank R^(k) (canonicalComb_card) and is prefix-support minimal (IsPrefixSupportMinimal = the span condition eq:ncg-prefix-minimality, canonicalComb_minimal); every realization has dim A_k >= rank R^(k) with equality iff prefix minimal (realizedChoi_rank_le_card, isPrefixSupportMinimal_iff_card_eq_rank); A_k = supp R^(k) via W_k (memory_equiv_support). Sequential uniqueness: sequentialComb_unitary_covariance - two prefix-support-minimal realizations of the same terminal tensor are related by memory unitaries U_k with U_0 = I, W'_k = (I (x) U_k) W_k and V'_k = (I (x) U_{k+1}) V_k (I (x) U_k)^H, the family being unique among isometric families with U_0 = I satisfying the link relation (proof: per-cut minimal-purification unitary exists_memory_unitary, then cancellation against the spanning input-extended prefix vectors). 'Same external port identifications' is rendered by the common slot types O, I. Consequence clause: the reduced primitive branch maps of fixed external interventions (SequentialIsometricComb.typedBranches) are Hilbert-Schmidt conjugated by the memory unitaries (typedBranches_op_covariant, from reducedKraus_covariant), the explicit HS unitary hsBlockUnitary U = (+)U_k (x) conj U_k conjugates the whole word representation, hence HS adjoints and all mixed history words (typedBranches_realization_independent, rep_eq_hsBlockUnitary_conj), and the word Grams of the future-separated history algebra coincide (histAlgebra_realization_independent). Scoped renderings (disclosed): uniform finite slot spaces at every slot; complex-multilinear tables; [Nonempty I] for the uniqueness clause; A_k identified with supp R^(k) (memory_equiv_support), which has the same dimension as the conjugate support named in the paper. Non-vacuity: qubitIdentityComb examples. |

| Proved (repair: realization independence no longer assumes the covariance). Representation part: process_history_representation (rho_X star hom on the free path star algebra, path relations, Ker rho a two-sided star ideal, F/Ker rho = C*(rho(a)) finite-dimensional and closed), instantiated on a sequential realization by SequentialIsometricComb.typedBranches (boundary objects = cuts k <= N with memories A_k; primitive branch = fixed external intervention at a slot, reduced Schroedinger map sum_nu K_nu (.) K_nu^H with K_nu = reducedKraus of the link). Independence derived: typedBranches_realization_independent - for two prefix-support-minimal realizations of the same terminal tensor, the proved sequential covariance (sequentialComb_unitary_covariance) gives memory unitaries, typedBranches_op_covariant transports the reduced maps, the explicit HS unitary hsBlockUnitary conjugates the whole word representation (rep_eq_hsBlockUnitary_conj) and preserves the causal readouts; kernels coincide and a represented star isomorphism exists (purification coordinates and the choice of realization). Tomographic frame: typedBranches_frame_independent (realizations of the tensors reconstructed from one table in two spanning physical frames; the tensors coincide by combFrameTensor_frame_independent), typedBranches_table_independent, typedBranches_physical_table_independent. Kraus coordinates: typedBranches_rep_eq_of_choi_eq via krausMap_reducedKraus (the reduced map depends only on the intervention's Choi matrix). Disclosed rendering: the paper's typed boundary objects are instantiated as the cuts of the support-minimal sequential realization; [Nonempty I]. |

| Proved. (1) Simultaneous finite unitary completion (exists_simultaneous_unitary_completion): for every finite family T_i : M_{n_i}(C) -> M_{m_i}(C) (arbitrary finite dimensions per map) of completely positive (library IsMatrixCompletelyPositive) and trace-nonincreasing (IsTraceNonincreasing: Re Tr T(X) <= Re Tr X for X >= 0) maps there is ONE unitary U on the finite space sum_i ((C^{m_i} (x) C^{kappa_i}) + C^{n_i}) (finite Kraus ancillas kappa_i = n_i m_i, finite control register i) with T_i(X) = sum_k O_{i,k} U J_i X J_i^H U^H O_{i,k}^H, where the input embeddings J_i = familyInput are isometries with mutually orthogonal ranges and the read-outs O_{i,k} = familyReadout are coisometries with orthogonal supports (tracing out the ancilla). Rendering of 'unitary completion after adjoining ancillas and a control register' as this explicit dilation formula is disclosed. Proof: Kraus form of CP maps (new MatrixKraus.exists_kraus_of_completelyPositive), sum W^H W <= 1 from trace-nonincreasingness, Stinespring isometry [W; D] with D^H D = 1 - sum W^H W, extension of the isometry to a unitary by orthonormal-basis extension (instead of the Julia formula; the statement is existential), and the block-diagonal controlled direct sum. (2) Isometry form, V : Matrix h k C with V^H V = 1, P = V V^H, Q = 1 - P, kappa(b) = V^H b V: kappa(ab) - kappa(a)kappa(b) = V^H a Q b V (compress_mul_sub_mul) and kappa(a^H a) - kappa(a)^H kappa(a) = (QaV)^H(QaV) >= 0 (compress_star_mul_sub_mul, compress_star_mul_sub_mul_posSemidef). (3) For a unital *-subalgebra C (StarSubalgebra of Matrix h h C; the ambient finite C*-algebra B plays no role), kappa is multiplicative on C iff P commutes with C (compress_multiplicative_iff_commute), and kappa restricted to C is a unital *-homomorphism iff P reduces C (exists_starAlgHom_iff_commute); 'P reduces C' is rendered as P commuting with every element, shown equivalent to Q c P = 0 for all c in C, i.e. invariance of Ran P (corners_eq_zero_iff_commute). Non-vacuity example (identity channel is CP and trace-nonincreasing) in the file. |
| `thm:ncg-contextual-envelope` | theorem | Universal future-separated history algebra | [`RenewalGeometry.contextualFutureNull`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualFutureNullIdeal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualFutureNullIdeal_isTwoSided`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualFutureNullIdeal_star_mem`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualHistoryBlockRepresentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualHistoryBlockRepresentation_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualHistoryBlockRepresentation_ker`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualHistoryBlockAlgebra_finiteDimensional`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualFutureNull_finiteMatrixBlock_quotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualNull`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualEnvelope.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextual_null_absorption`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualEnvelope.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextual_envelope_universal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualEnvelope.lean) | Same content as emergent_spacetime lem:supp-contextual-ideal (proved; same Lean). N^fut with both test families R(uqv) = R(uq^*v) = 0 is contextualFutureNull; it is a two-sided ideal (contextualFutureNullIdeal, contextualFutureNullIdeal_isTwoSided) and star-stable (contextualFutureNullIdeal_star_mem). For the finite process algebra in matrix-block form the quotient A^hist is realised concretely as the surviving-block C*-algebra with a surjective star-homomorphism whose kernel is exactly N^fut (contextualHistoryBlockRepresentation_surjective / _ker, finite dimensional, central-projection decomposition contextualFutureNull_finiteMatrixBlock_quotient). Universality: contextual_envelope_universal proves that for any surjection pi through which every readout factors, Ker pi lies in the null space, every map killing the null space factors through pi, and the factoring map is injective when the quotient is separated, i.e. the quotient is canonically identified. Disclosed instantiation: contextual_envelope_universal is stated for a family of additive readouts with the single test family; the paper's two-family nullity and the separation condition eq:ncg-future-separated-definition are its instance at the doubled readout family {R, R^dagger}, R^dagger(x) = conj R(x^*), whose single-family null space is contextualFutureNull R; this instantiation, and the statements 'A^hist carries every readout / is future separated' (the readouts vanish on N^fut, and the separation argument inside the proof of contextual_envelope_universal), are not separate lemmas. |
| `thm:ncg-flat-history-reconstruction` | theorem | Finite flat reconstruction of the history algebra | [`RenewalGeometry.flat_word_reconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.exists_starAlgEquiv_of_panel_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.panelEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.wordCombination_surjective_of_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ker_wordCombination_eq_of_panel_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.genElt_mul_wordCombination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.star_wordCombination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.flat_word_reconstruction_rank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordGramPositivityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rank_gramPanel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordGramPositivityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gramPanel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordGramPositivityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGrandTensor.exists_least_flat_depth`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGrandTensor.historyWordSpan_eq_historyAlgebra_of_flat`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGrandTensor.wordGramLevel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteGrandTensorExact.lean) | Same content as emergent_spacetime thm:supp-word-flatness (proved; same Lean). For a represented generator family on a nonempty finite carrier closed under a letter involution: there is a least r_X with rank K_{X,r_X} = rank K_{X,r_X+1} (flat_word_reconstruction, flat_word_reconstruction_rank; ranks = word-span dimensions, rank_gramPanel); at that depth the words of length <= r_X span A^hist (historyWordSpan_eq_historyAlgebra_of_flat); and for any second family over the same alphabet with equal depth-(r+1) panel and gen flat at depth r (flatness then transfers through the depth-r sub-panel) there is a star-algebra isomorphism psi with psi[w] = [w]' for every word w (exists_starAlgEquiv_of_panel_eq; multiplication from genElt_mul_wordCombination, involution from star_wordCombination). ntialIsometricCombRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGrandTensor.grand_tensor_reconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteGrandTensorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextual_envelope_universal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualEnvelope.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.contextualFutureNullIdeal_star_mem`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ContextualFutureNullIdeal.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.flat_word_reconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.flat_word_reconstruction_rank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordGramPositivityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.normalizedRegularTrace_algEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/IntrinsicRegularTrace.lean) | Proved by one composite theorem (repair of the missing composition): grand_tensor_reconstruction_of_statistics - for two finite operational processes over the same event grammar with equal complete intervention statistics (equal probabilities of every tuple of trace-normalized positive probes at every depth k <= N) and ANY prefix-support-minimal sequential realizations C, C' of their tensors: (i) the predictive laws coincide (hence Z^min, branch probabilities and partial updates; universality of Z^min by PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique etc., bundled in FiniteGrandTensor.grand_tensor_reconstruction); (ii) the represented process algebras are star-isomorphic by a represented isomorphism and the universal future-separated history algebras A^proc/N^fut are star-isomorphic by an isomorphism carrying every history word to its namesake; (iii) normalized regular traces correspond and every word-Gram layer coincides; (iv) the flat criterion rank K_r = rank K_{r+1} holds at exactly the same depths, and there is a least flat depth at which the words span A^hist (flat_word_reconstruction_rank); the panel reconstruction of unit, multiplication and involution at that depth is thm:ncg-flat-history-reconstruction (proved, flat_word_reconstruction, applicable since histGen is star-closed and A^hist is nonzero). Chain: multilinear_eq_of_physical -> equal tensors -> sequentialComb_unitary_covariance -> typedBranches_realization_independent with causal-readout invariance -> equal future-null kernels (histMap_eq_zero_iff_of_realizations) -> exists_starAlgEquiv_of_ker_eq -> normalizedRegularTrace_algEquiv. Grand-tensor form: GrandTensor.ofProcess_eq_of_statistics. Parents thm:ncg-comb-tomography, thm:ncg-process-history-representation (this batch) and thm:ncg-contextual-envelope, thm:ncg-flat-history-reconstruction (proved). Disclosed renderings as in def:ncg-grand-tensor (causal readouts, uniform slot spaces, common Kraus index type). Non-vacuity: qubitIdentityProcess example. |

m`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.blockUnit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.card_eq_of_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean) | Proved (operational_realization). Input: a faithful unital representation rendered as an injective star-algebra hom pi : A ->*a[C] Matrix n n C (A any complex *-algebra; injectivity + finite n make A a finite-dimensional C*-algebra; any finite carrier index n, Wedderburn form via exists_wedderburn_blockForm = reciprocal_wedderburn after reindexing). Output: a finite memory C^Omega with \|Omega\| = dim K and a valid Scheme (IsValid: density preparations, effect readouts, CP branches in finite Kraus form IsKrausCP, equivalent to IsMatrixCompletelyPositive for Fin d, and normalized two-outcome instruments Tr(succ X + fail X) = Tr X) such that Scheme.RealizesRep pi: the internal history algebra A^proc (star subalgebra of B(HS(C^Omega)) generated by the HS matrices of all branches; star = HS adjoint by superMatrix_eq_conjTranspose_of_hsAdjoint) has a surjective *-hom theta onto A whose kernel is exactly the contextual future-null ideal of the boundary readouts Tr(E_i x(rho_j)) (contextualFutureNullIdeal, both q and q* tested), i.e. the future-separated quotient is A, and an HS unitary T : C^n -> span{rho_j} with x(Tv) = T(pi(theta x) v) (so the source span is reducing, RealizesRep.reducing, and the source representation is unitarily equivalent to pi with all multiplicities). Construction exactly as in the proof: Wedderburn form via reciprocal_wedderburn (W unitary, W^H pi(A) W = (+) M_{n_l} (x) I_{m_l}), basis e_{l,a,r}, p_{l,a,r} = \|e><e\| as preparations and effects, success branches Phi^l_ab = sum_r L X L^H with L = \|e_{l,a,r}><e_{l,b,r}\| (realizationScheme_succ_eq_kraus), failure branches P - Phi^l_bb with P the dephasing channel (realizationScheme_fail_eq); the general mechanism is realizesRep_of_frame (compression to the reducing HS frame is a *-hom, compressHom/range_compressHom, readouts = compressed matrix coefficients). Instruments are two-outcome (success/failure) indexed by (l,a,b). |
| `lem:ncg-rank-one-forcing` | lemma | Rank-one forcing from a deterministic transition panel | [`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.rankOneForcing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.IsDensity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.IsEffect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.trace_mul_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.mul_eq_zero_of_perfectDiscrimination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.sum_rank_le_card_of_mul_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.sum_rank_le_card`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.rank_eq_one_of_card_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.exists_unit_vector_of_rank_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.exists_orthonormal_of_card_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.eq_sum_of_offDiag_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.panel_invariant_kraus`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixKraus.exists_kraus_of_completelyPositive`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/MatrixKrausExistence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixKraus.eq_krausMap_conjTranspose_of_hsAdjoint`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/MatrixKrausExistence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MatrixKraus.hsInner_krausMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/MatrixKrausExistence.lean) | Proved (rankOneForcing bundles all clauses, H = C^d). Densities: PSD with trace 1 (IsDensity); effects: 0 <= E <= 1 (IsEffect); perfect discrimination Tr(E_i rho_j) = delta_ij. (a) sum_j rank rho_j <= dim H (sum_rank_le_card, any finite index type): Tr(AB)=0 iff AB=0 for PSD A,B gives rho_i rho_j = 0 for i != j, and ranges of Hermitian matrices with pairwise zero products are independent (finrank_iSup_range_eq_sum). (b) If dim H = N: every rho_j has rank one (rank_eq_one_of_card_eq) and rho_j = \|e_j><e_j\| for an orthonormal family e (exists_orthonormal_of_card_eq), i.e. rank-one states with mutually orthogonal supports. (c) For Phi completely positive (library IsMatrixCompletelyPositive on M_d) with Phi(rho_j) = w_j rho_{sigma(j)} (w_j > 0) for j in J, Tr Phi(rho_j) = 0 for j not in J, sigma injective on J (sigma rendered as a map iota -> iota with Set.InjOn sigma J, equivalent to sigma : J -> {1..N} injective), the span V = span{rho_j} is invariant under Phi and under EVERY Hilbert-Schmidt adjoint Psi of Phi (any linear Psi with <Y, Phi X>_HS = <Psi Y, X>_HS; it is shown to equal the adjoint Kraus map). Infrastructure: CP => Kraus (MatrixKraus.exists_kraus_of_completelyPositive, exported from the Choi criterion), Kraus HS adjoint and its uniqueness, Kraus-level panel invariance (panel_invariant_kraus, general finite types) following the paper's argument (K_nu e_j = 0 off J, K_nu e_j in C e_sigma(j) on J, off-diagonal HS-adjoint coefficients vanish by injectivity). The hypothesis w_j > 0 is not needed. Non-vacuity example (C^1, identity channel) in the file. |
| `prop:ncg-operational-recognition` | proposition | Finite recognition from the one-step panel | [`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.operational_recognition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.Scheme.SamePanel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.card_le_of_perfectDiscrimination`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.frame_data_of_card_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.eff_eq_prep_of_card_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.realizationMatrix_partialPerm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.realization_image_eq_range`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.Scheme.realizesRep_of_frame`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.Scheme.RealizesRep`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.panel_invariant_kraus`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.exists_orthonormal_of_card_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RankOneForcing.sum_rank_le_card`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RankOneForcing.lean) | Proved (operational_recognition). For every faithful unital *-representation pi : A -> M_n(C) the realization scheme S of thm:ncg-operational-realization (valid, memory dimension n, S.RealizesRep pi) satisfies: every valid scheme S' on any finite memory C^Omega' (same preparation index and instrument family) reproducing the retained panel of S (Scheme.SamePanel: the perfect boundary discrimination table Tr(E_i rho_j) and ALL one-step success and complementary failure transition probabilities Tr(E_i Phi_gamma(rho_j)), which include the zero probabilities outside each branch domain) has memory dimension >= n (card_le_of_perfectDiscrimination, eq:perfect-discrimination-rank) and, when its dimension is minimal (= n, attained by S), S'.RealizesRep pi: its future-separated source representation (internal history algebra modulo the contextual future-null ideal, acting on span{rho'_j}) is unitarily equivalent to (A, C^n, pi). Proof as in the paper: at minimal dimension the rho'_j are rank-one projectors on an orthonormal basis and E'_i = rho'_i (eff_eq_prep_of_card_eq); PSD outputs with prescribed diagonal transition weights are forced to Phi'(rho'_j) = sum_i A_ij rho'_i; lem:ncg-rank-one-forcing (panel_invariant_kraus) gives reducing for the HS adjoint since every retained branch has a partial-injection successor rule (realizationMatrix_partialPerm); the retained transition matrices are the same E^(l)_ab (x) I and 1 - E^(l)_bb (x) I, so realizesRep_of_frame applies with the same Wedderburn unitary. Rendering: the separate total probabilities Tr Phi(rho_j) are not needed as extra hypotheses (implied at minimal dimension since E'_i = rho'_i sum to 1), so SamePanel omits them (stronger statement). |
| `prop:ncg-boundary-enlargement` | proposition | — | [`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.reset`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.resetAdjoint`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.hsInner_reset`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.resetAdjoint_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.hsInner_diagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.reset_diagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.resetAdjoint_diagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.resetSourceMatrix_mul_conjTranspose`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.reset_resetAdjoint_diagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.resetProducts_represent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.adjoin_resetProducts_eq_top`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.adjoin_sup_resetProducts_eq_top`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.lt_adjoin_sup_resetProducts`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BoundaryReset.exists_proper_subalgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/BoundaryResetEnlargement.lean) | Proved. Memory C^N (n a finite type, N = card n), distinguished source preparations p_u = \|e_u><e_u\| (diagonal matrix units, i.e. the rank-one preparations on an orthonormal basis, coordinates WLOG), memory operators with the HS inner product Tr(X^H Y). reset u is the superoperator R_u(X) = Tr(X) p_u; resetAdjoint u (Y -> Y_uu 1) is its HS adjoint (hsInner_reset) and the unique one (resetAdjoint_unique). The source span V = span{p_u} is the image of the HS isometry x -> diagonal x (hsInner_diagonal); on it R_u acts as \|e_u><1\| (reset_diagonal), R_u^* as \|1><e_u\| (resetAdjoint_diagonal) and R_u R_v^* as N \|e_u><e_v\| (reset_resetAdjoint_diagonal, resetSourceMatrix_mul_conjTranspose, resetProducts_represent). adjoin_resetProducts_eq_top: these source-span matrices generate M_N(C) as a C-algebra (hence also as a *-algebra). 'Thus a proper block algebra can be destroyed': adjoining them to any subalgebra gives all of M_N(C) (adjoin_sup_resetProducts_eq_top), so every proper subalgebra (e.g. a proper block algebra sum M_{n_lambda} (x) I_{m_lambda}) is strictly enlarged (lt_adjoin_sup_resetProducts); for N >= 2 proper subalgebras exist (exists_proper_subalgebra, the scalar block algebra). The generation itself holds for every N >= 1; N >= 2 is only needed for properness. No hypothesis packet (no vacuity issue). |

| Proved (enrichedClosure). Rendering: the memory HS space is the finite Hilbert space m -> C with the standard inner product (for B(H) take m = H x H; vectorisation is an HS isometry), L : Matrix m r C has the states rho_j as columns (L x = sum x_j rho_j), Phi a : Matrix m m C the branch superoperators, R : Matrix s m C the retained response map; G = L^H L, H_a = L^H Phi_a L, K_a = (Phi_a L)^H (Phi_a L), B = R L, C_a = R Phi_a L, B^dagger = B^H (B B^H)^{-1} (responsePinv; responsePinv_isMoorePenrose proves it is the Moore-Penrose inverse of a full-row-rank B), A_a = C_a B^dagger. Hypotheses exactly as stated: columns of L linearly independent, rows of R L linearly independent (full row rank), K_a - H_a^H G^{-1} H_a = 0 and C_a (1 - B^dagger B) = 0 for every branch. Conclusions: Ran L forward invariant under every Phi_a; R Phi_a X = A_a R X for X in Ran L; R Phi_w L = A_w B for every finite word w (ordered List products of Phi and A in the same order); plus uniqueness of A_a (the factorization through the response coordinates is unique). Proof ingredients also exported: P_V = L G^{-1} L^H is a Hermitian idempotent fixing L, the Gram-defect identity K_a - H_a^H G^{-1} H_a = ((1-P_V) Phi_a L)^H ((1-P_V) Phi_a L) (successorGram_sub_eq), PSD-ness, and the iff with forward invariance (successorGram_sub_eq_zero_iff_forwardInvariant). The remarks on measuring overlaps via the swap identity and on 'additional resources' are interpretive, not formalised. Non-vacuity example in the file (one state, identity branch and response). |
| `cor:ncg-operational-calibrated-triples` | corollary | — | [`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.operational_calibrated_triples`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalRealization.operational_realization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/FiniteOperationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedCommutantCoordinates.MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple.commutant_fibre`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedCommutantCoordinates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedCommutantCoordinates.MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple.eq_dirac_of_commutantCoordinates_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedCommutantCoordinates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedCommutantCoordinates.MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple.exists_orthonormal_commutant_family`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedCommutantCoordinates.lean) | Proved (operational_calibrated_triples), parents thm:ncg-operational-realization (proved in this batch) and thm:commutant-fibre (proved). For every MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple S (def:marked-spectral-triple encoding): (1) the represented algebra component pi(A) = S.rep.range, faithfully and unitally represented by inclusion (StarSubalgebra.subtype), admits the operational realization (valid Scheme, memory dimension \|K\|, RealizesRep of the inclusion), by operational_realization; (2) after supplying the commutator derivation and declared marks (compatible implementers, IsCompatibleImplementer) and the sharp real-linear commutant coordinates of thm:commutant-fibre, the full compatible Dirac operator is reconstructed: q_S HS-orthonormal commutant coordinates exist, equal coordinates force X = D, any injective real-linear coordinate map on c_S works, and fewer than q_S coordinates never suffice (clauses of commutant_fibre). The disclaimer sentence has no mathematical content. Non-vacuity example on trivialMarkedFiniteSpectralTriple included. |
| `thm:supp-dynamic-jets` | theorem | Static self-energy, inverse moments, and positive Euclidean memory | [`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.dynamic_jets`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDynamicJetsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.posSemidef_matrixIm_stieltjesSelfEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureIntegralCalculus.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.tendsto_stieltjesSelfEnergy_neg_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDynamicJetsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.iteratedDerivWithin_stieltjesSelfEnergy_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDynamicJetsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.stieltjesSelfEnergy_neg_eq_integral_euclideanMemoryKernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDynamicJetsExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.euclideanMemoryKernel_eq_compress`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VectorMeasureCalculus.hasDerivWithinAt_integral_of_dominated`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/VectorMeasureParametricIntegral.lean) | Proved for general positive operator-valued tail measures (def:supp-POVM, PositiveTailMeasure), packaged in dynamic_jets: (i) eq:supp-Herglotz-sign: Im Σ_μ(z) := (Σ-Σᴴ)/(2i) = (Im z) ∫\|λ-z\|^{-2} dμ ⪰ 0 for Im z > 0; (ii) eq:supp-static-inverse-moment: lim_{s↓0} Σ_μ(-s) exists iff ∫ λ^{-1} dTr μ < ∞ (both directions; necessity via monotone convergence of the trace), and then the limit is ∫ λ^{-1} dμ; (iii) eq:supp-dynamic-jet: if ∫ λ^{-(r+1)} dTr μ < ∞ then the r-th one-sided derivative of x ↦ Σ_μ(x) within (-∞,0] at 0 equals r! ∫ λ^{-(r+1)} dμ (derivatives taken entrywise, i.e. in the normed space H → H → ℂ via Matrix.of.symm, since Matrix carries no global norm); (iv) K_μ(t) = ∫ e^{-tλ} dμ ⪰ 0 for t ≥ 0, the Laplace relation Σ_μ(-s) = ∫_0^∞ e^{-st} K_μ(t) dt for s > 0 (Bochner integral of the entries; Tonelli on the scalarisations + polarisation), and the dilation form K_μ(t) = B_μ e^{-tC_μ} B_μ^* (euclideanMemoryKernel_eq_compress in the Naimark file, with e^{-tC_μ} the multiplication operator by e^{-tλ} on K_μ). Scoped choices disclosed: the jets are stated for the restriction of Σ_μ to the real axis (the paper's Σ^{(r)}_{μ,-}(0)), and the Laplace relation for s > 0. New reusable infrastructure: differentiation under the vector-measure integral sign (VectorMeasureCalculus.hasDerivWithinAt_integral_of_dominated), adjoint/Hermitian/psd calculus for operator integrals (OperatorTailMeasureIntegralCalculus), Laplace/Stieltjes Fubini relation (StieltjesLaplaceScalar). |

[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.memory_determinacy_of_moment_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.eq_of_isSupportedIn_of_moment_eq'`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.eq_of_integrable_exp_traceMeasure_of_moment_eq'`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.isSupportedIn_of_moment_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.integrable_exp_traceMeasure_of_moment_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.integrable_pow_variation_of_moment_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.re_trace_moment`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.lintegral_exp_eq_tsum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.atomic_isSupportedIn`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyTransfer.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.eq_of_isSupportedIn_of_moment_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.eq_of_integrable_exp_traceMeasure_of_moment_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.measure_eq_of_forall_integral_pow_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureDeterminacyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.moment`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureStieltjesExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.IsSupportedIn`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureStieltjesExact.lean) | Proved with hypotheses on mu only (memory_determinacy_of_moment_eq): (compact) if mu is supported in (0,R], every positive operator-valued tail measure mu' with the same moment sequence M_n equals mu; (exponential) if int e^{a lambda} dTr mu < inf for some a > 0, the same holds. The hypothesis on mu' is derived, not assumed: integrable_pow_variation_of_moment_eq shows that equal moments force all powers to be mu'-integrable (the Bochner junk value M_n(mu') = 0 for a non-integrable power would give int lambda^n dTr mu = 0, hence Tr mu = 0, hence Tr mu'(R) = Re Tr M_0(mu') = 0); re_trace_moment identifies Re Tr M_n with int lambda^n dTr; isSupportedIn_of_moment_eq is Chebyshev Tr mu'([T,inf)) <= (R/T)^{2n} Tr mu(R) -> 0 for every T > max R 0; integrable_exp_traceMeasure_of_moment_eq is Tonelli: int^- e^{a lambda} dTr mu' = sum_n a^n/n! int lambda^n dTr mu' = the same series for mu (lintegral_exp_eq_tsum). Determinacy itself is the earlier scalar MGF/identity-theorem argument plus polarisation. Rendering: 'supp mu compact' = IsSupportedIn R for some R (mu lives on (0,inf) by def:supp-POVM); H finite-dimensional as in the file's def:supp-POVM encoding. Non-vacuity: atomic_isSupportedIn and an example with the atom 1*delta_1 on C^2. |
| `cth:supp-moment-indeterminate` | proposition | — | [`RenewalGeometry.LognormalIndeterminacy.moment_indeterminate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/LognormalMomentIndeterminacyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LognormalIndeterminacy.integral_zpow_lognormalMeasure`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/LognormalMomentIndeterminacyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LognormalIndeterminacy.exists_isOpen_stieltjesTransform_ne`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/LognormalMomentIndeterminacyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StieltjesLaplace.measure_eq_of_forall_integral_inv_add_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/StieltjesLaplaceScalar.lean) | Proved. lognormalMeasure ε encodes eq:supp-lognormal-family as the measure [1+ε sin(2π log x)] e^{-(log x)^2/2}/(x√(2π)) dx on (0,∞). integral_zpow_lognormalMeasure: ∫ x^n dμ_ε = e^{n^2/2} for every n ∈ ℤ and \|ε\| ≤ 1 (substitution x = e^y via integral_image_eq_integral_abs_deriv_smul, completing the square, shift y ↦ y+n and oddness). exists_isOpen_stieltjesTransform_ne: for ε ≠ ε' the scalar Stieltjes transforms differ on a nonempty open subset of ℂ∖[0,∞); it rests on the new reusable injectivity theorem StieltjesLaplace.measure_eq_of_forall_integral_inv_add_eq (finite measures on [0,∞) with equal Stieltjes transform on the negative axis coincide), proved exactly as in the paper: Fubini relation ∫(λ+s)^{-1}dν = ∫_0^∞ e^{-st}L_ν(t)dt and two applications of Laplace uniqueness (Laplace uniqueness at the integers is derived from the library's compact moment determinacy by pushing forward under x ↦ e^{-x}). The 'consequently' clause (positive moments, inverse moments, Hankel panels agree) is the n ∈ ℤ moment identity, since each of those quantities is a function of the integer moment sequence. |

| `thm:supp-contact-escape` | theorem | High-energy memory escape and instantaneous contact terms | [`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.contact_escape`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureContactEscapeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.tendstoLocallyUniformlyOn_stieltjesSelfEnergy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureContactEscapeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.tendsto_integral_smul_euclideanMemoryKernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureContactEscapeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.escapeMeasure`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureContactEscapeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.finitePart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureContactEscapeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.integral_smul_integral_kernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureContactEscapeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorMeasureL2.tendstoUniformlyOn_of_lipschitzOnWith`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureContactEscapeExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorMeasureL2.exists_subseq_tendsto_posOperatorMeasure`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OperatorMeasureWeakCompactness.lean) | Proved (contact_escape). Hypotheses: mu n : PositiveTailMeasure H supported in [m,infty) (IsSupportedAbove m, m > 0) and Re Tr Sigma_n(0) <= C for all n (Sigma_n(0) = nu_n([m,infty)) with nu_n = lambda^{-1} d mu_n, escapeMeasure_univ). Conclusion: a strictly increasing phi and a positive operator-valued measure nulim on the one-point compactification [m,infty] realised as EscapeSpace m = Icc 0 (1/m) via y = 1/lambda (infinity = y = 0) such that (i) the push-forwards escapeMeasure of lambda^{-1} d mu_{phi n} converge weakly to nulim against every bounded continuous g; (ii) Q_esc := nulim({infty}) is psd and nu_fin := finitePart nulim (the restriction of nulim to (0,1/m] pulled back to [m,infty), a PositiveTailMeasure supported in [m,infty), with the explicit set identity nu_fin(s) = nulim(s^{-1} cap {infty}^c), i.e. nulim = nu_fin + Q_esc delta_infty); (iii) eq:supp-contact-selfenergy: Sigma_{phi n}(z) -> Q_esc + int lambda/(lambda - z) d nu_fin locally uniformly on C minus [m,infty) (TendstoLocallyUniformlyOn on escapeDomain m); (iv) eq:supp-contact-kernel: for every bounded continuous test function psi, int_0^infty psi(t) K_{phi n}(t) dt -> psi(0) Q_esc + int_0^infty psi(t) K_fin(t) dt with K_fin(t) = int lambda e^{-lambda t} d nu_fin(lambda) (distributional convergence K_n dt -> Q_esc delta_0 + K_fin dt), proved through the test function h_psi(y) = int psi(s y) e^{-s} ds on the compactification and a Fubini lemma for operator-valued Euclidean kernels (integral_smul_integral_kernel). Infrastructure built: scalarisation bridge for positive operator-valued measures on a general measurable space (traceMeasure, quadForm_integral), real/imaginary splitting of complex operator integrals, change of variables y = 1/lambda for lambda^{-1} d mu (integral_escapeMeasure, via Mathlib's VectorMeasure.withDensity and VectorMeasure.map), uniform Lipschitz bounds on compacta and an epsilon/3-net lemma (tendstoUniformlyOn_of_lipschitzOnWith). Only propext/Classical.choice/Quot.sound. |

n)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.TreeGauge.loopGraph_card_chords`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleSpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree.bfs`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree.nonempty`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree.parent_injective`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree.card_treeFinset`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree.card_chords_add_card`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree.card_chords_eq_finrank_H1`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.SpanningTree.connectedTo`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`NCG.Multigraph.Walk.length`](../../NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.marked_spectral_bundle_descent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.holonomy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.holonomy_gaugeAct_loop`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.globallyTrivial_iff_holonomy_eq_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.gaugeEquivalent_iff_holonomy_conj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean) | Proved. Spanning-tree infrastructure for NCG.Multigraph (NCG/Graph/SpanningTree.lean): SpanningTree G v0 is a rooted spanning tree in breadth-first form (depth vanishing exactly at v0, parent edge to a vertex of depth one less, tree paths extending the parent path); SpanningTree.bfs constructs one on every graph connected to v0 (BFS on walk distance); parent edges are injective, so a finite connected multigraph has \|V\|-1 tree edges (card_treeFinset) and \|E\|-\|V\|+1 chords, equal to b1 = dim H^1(G;Z/2) (card_chords_add_card, card_chords_eq_finrank_H1, via finrank_H1_add_card_vertices). On top (MarkedSpectralBundleSpanningTree.lean, G_S modelled by an arbitrary group as in the existing file): the tree frame g(v) = hol_U(tree path)^{-1} gauge-fixes U to be trivial on T with chord labels = fundamental-cycle holonomies (isTreeNormal_gaugeAct_treeGauge, gaugeAct_treeGauge_apply); (B1) rho_U : FreeGroup(chords) ->* G_S sends a chord to its fundamental-cycle holonomy, and every closed-walk monodromy factors as hol_U(p) = rho_U(chordWord p) (holonomy_eq_rho_chordWord), where chordWord is multiplicative, inverted by reversal, backtracking-invariant and surjective from closed walks at v0 (chordWord_surjective); (B2) rho_gaugeAct; (B3) isGloballyTrivial_iff_rho_eq_one; (B4) gaugeEquivalent_iff_rho_conj; final sentence: tree-normal bundles are gauge equivalent iff their chord tuples are simultaneously conjugate (gaugeEquivalent_iff_of_isTreeNormal), every chord tuple is realised by exactly one tree-normal bundle (existsUnique_isTreeNormal), and gaugeClassEquiv is an explicit bijection {flat marked bundles}/gauge ~= (chords -> G_S)/simultaneous conjugation, with #chords = b1. Rendering disclosed: pi_1(Gamma) is not defined as a group in the library; its role is played by the free group on the b1 chords together with the surjective, multiplicative, backtracking-invariant chord-word map from closed walks (the classical identification pi_1 ~= F(chords), i.e. injectivity of chordWord modulo backtracking, which the paper cites as 'pi_1 is free of rank b1', is not formalised; the classification of bundles by b1-tuples modulo simultaneous conjugation is proved directly and does not depend on it). Non-vacuity: the one-loop graph has exactly one chord (loopGraph_card_chords). |
| `cor:supp-twisted-bundle` | corollary | — | [`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.IsFlatTwisted`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.isFlatTwisted_of_holonomy_ne_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.isFlatTwisted_of_class_nontrivial`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedBundle.monodromy_class_trivial_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralBundleDescentExact.lean) | Re-verified against the new statement: the corollary now asserts directly that nontrivial [rho_U] obstructs a global marked trivialization compatible with the edge identifications (no longer phrased through 'flat twisted spectral geometry'). Lean: IsFlatTwisted U is by definition the negation of IsGloballyTrivial U (a frame change making every edge map the identity, i.e. a global trivialization compatible with all edge identifications), and isFlatTwisted_of_holonomy_ne_one / isFlatTwisted_of_class_nontrivial prove that a nontrivial monodromy (some closed walk with holonomy != 1, equivalently a nontrivial conjugacy class, monodromy_class_trivial_iff) forces it: the contrapositive of (B3). The compatibility relation U(bar e) = U(e)^{-1} is built into the walk holonomy. Same modelling disclosures as thm:supp-marked-bundle. Audit 2 Oct 2026: thm:supp-marked-bundle is conditional_interface only for the spanning-tree / chord normal form; the clause used here, (B3), is proved, and rho_U is rendered by walk holonomy (no chord presentation needed). |
| `thm:character-holonomy-detection` | theorem | — | [`RenewalGeometry.MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadow.characters_trivial_of_holonomy_trivial`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadowExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadow.holonomy_trivial_of_characters_trivial`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadowExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadow.characters_detect_holonomy_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadowExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadow.characters_detect_holonomy_iff_autGroup`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadowExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadow.characters_trivial_of_holonomy_trivial_autGroup`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadowExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadow.jointCharacterKernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedSpectralPacketCharacterShadowExact.lean) | Relabelled from thm:supp-character-shadow; re-verified against the new statement: the converse is now quantified 'for all such graph bundles'. This is exactly what the Lean gives: (ii) a trivial joint kernel forces rho = 1 for every monodromy of every graph (any group), and conversely, if the converse holds for all graph bundles it holds in particular on the one-cycle graph (pi_1 = free group of rank 1), where characters_detect_holonomy_iff (Nonempty beta) forces the joint kernel to be trivial. Proved: (i) trivial frame holonomy ρ = 1 forces every induced character holonomy χ_j ∘ ρ, ε_k ∘ ρ to be trivial (arbitrary groups); (ii) if ⋂_j Ker χ_j ∩ ⋂_k Ker ε_k = {1} then trivial character holonomies force ρ = 1 for every monodromy ρ from any group; (iii) the 'exactly when': for monodromies of the free fundamental group π_1(Γ) = FreeGroup β on b_1(Γ) ≥ 1 generators (Nonempty β), the characters detect every frame holonomy iff the joint kernel is trivial (the paper's one-cycle construction, via FreeGroup.lift). Specialised to G_S = autGroup of a marked packet with U(1)-valued characters (Circle) and Pfaffian-sign characters (ℤˣ). Scoped hypotheses: b_1 ≥ 1 for the 'only if' direction (for b_1 = 0 every ρ is trivial and the criterion is vacuous); π_1(Γ) is taken as the abstract free group of rank b_1, as in the paper; the graph monodromy ρ_U of thm:supp-marked-bundle is an arbitrary homomorphism here. Audit 2 Oct 2026: no dependence on the open spanning-tree normal form of thm:supp-marked-bundle; rho is an arbitrary homomorphism. |
| `cth:supp-det-no-frame` | proposition | — | [`RenewalGeometry.su2DiagonalMonodromy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.det_su2DiagonalMonodromy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.su2DiagonalMonodromy_mem_unitaryGroup`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.su2DiagonalMonodromy_comm_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.su2DiagonalMonodromy_comm_scalar`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.su2DiagonalMonodromy_ne_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.detCharacter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oneCycleMonodromy`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oneCycleMonodromy_det_trivial`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oneCycleMonodromy_ne_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean) | Concrete SU(2) computation. g = diagonal ![exp(iθ), exp(-iθ)] : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℂ has det g = 1, lies in Matrix.unitaryGroup (Fin 2) ℂ (the marked automorphism group U(2) of a scalar-algebra, zero-Dirac, multiplicity-two packet), commutes with D = 0 and with every scalar c•1, and g ≠ 1 when θ ≠ 2πn for all n : ℤ. The one-cycle graph has π₁ = Multiplicative ℤ; oneCycleMonodromy θ = zpowersHom g is the marked monodromy, its composite with the determinant character detCharacter : U(2) →* ℂˣ is the trivial homomorphism (trivial determinant-line holonomy), while oneCycleMonodromy θ ≠ 1 for θ ∉ 2πℤ (nontrivial marked bundle monodromy, via (B3), the proved clause of thm:supp-marked-bundle). Scoped choice disclosed: the marked automorphism group is modelled as the full unitary group U(2) ⊇ SU(2) of the packet's Hilbert carrier, and π₁ of the one-cycle graph as Multiplicative ℤ. Audit 2 Oct 2026: only the proved (B3) clause of thm:supp-marked-bundle is invoked. |
| `thm:supp-monodromy-descent` | theorem | Exact marked-monodromy descent | [`RenewalGeometry.monodromy_descends_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.monodromyDescent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.monodromyDescent_comp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.monodromyDescent_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.monodromyDescent_conj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.monodromyDescent_comp_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ker_le_ker_character_comp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.detCharacter_descends_but_monodromy_does_not`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean) | Abstract group-theoretic form, with π₁(Γ), π₁(Γ̄), G_S arbitrary groups and q_* any surjective homomorphism (the graph-contraction origin of q_* is not modelled; the theorem only uses surjectivity). monodromy_descends_iff: (∃! ρ̄, ρ = ρ̄ ∘ q_*) ↔ ker q_* ≤ ker ρ. monodromyDescent is the descended ρ̄ with monodromyDescent_comp (factorisation) and monodromyDescent_unique. Gauge independence: monodromyDescent_conj shows conjugating ρ by g ∈ G_S conjugates ρ̄ by the same g, so [ρ̄] is gauge independent. Functoriality: monodromyDescent_comp_surjective shows descent along q₁ then q₂ equals descent along q₂ ∘ q₁. Character remark: ker_le_ker_character_comp (a character of a descending ρ descends) and detCharacter_descends_but_monodromy_does_not (the one-cycle SU(2) example of cth:supp-det-no-frame: collapsing the cycle to a point, the determinant character satisfies the descended kernel condition while the full marked monodromy does not). |

etry.OperatorTailMeasure.IsMinimalRealization.finrank_range_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureCompressionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.memory_rg_semigroup`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/MemoryCutoffSemigroupMeasureExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CurrentChain.VertexQuotient.entropyProduction_edgeSum_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CurrentChainFunctorExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.monodromy_descends_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MarkedMonodromyDescentExact.lean) | Re-verified against the new statement: the packet display is now eq:combined-inverse-data (J(G,c) in place of Curr(G,c)); the finite static inverse moment of thm:supp-memory-RG is an explicit hypothesis (matching the integrability hypothesis of dynamicFunction_rg_zero used for the zero-energy clause); 'canonical and composes strictly' is weakened to 'the three components are canonical and compose componentwise', with nested isometries V1:H1->H0, V2:H2->H1 composed as V1V2 (CoarseGraining.comp uses W1 W2 and image_comp proves the componentwise composition law); the Pontryagin negative-square clause has been removed from the statement, so negSquares_compress_le is dropped from the citations. Proved as one composite theorem (universality_packet_functor) over the bundled packet. UniversalityPacket = (oriented graph with positive conductances, (A, μ) with μ a positive tail measure, marked monodromy ρ : F →* G) (eq:supp-U-packet); CoarseGraining = (vertex quotient with connected fibres retaining inter-fibre edge classes, threshold Λ > 0, isometric head map W with Wᴴ W = 1, surjective contraction q_* with ker q_* ≤ ker ρ); CoarseGraining.image is eq:supp-U-map componentwise (Q_E Curr, (W^*(A − Q_{>Λ})W, W^* μ\|_{(0,Λ]} W), ρ̄ = monodromyDescent). Clauses: (1) canonical — ρ̄ is the unique factorisation ρ = ρ̄ ∘ q_* (the other components are explicit formulas); (2) composes strictly — CoarseGraining.comp (VertexQuotient.comp with fibresConnected_comp/retains, W₁W₂, q₂∘q₁, threshold Λ₂) satisfies (d₁.comp d₂).image = d₂.image for nested thresholds Λ₂ ≤ Λ₁ (image_comp, from edgeMatrix_comp, rg_rg + rg_compress + compress_compress, monodromyDescent_comp_surjective); (3) currents map to currents and entropy production cannot increase; (4) zero-energy Schur complement preserved before compression (dynamicFunction_rg_zero, assuming λ⁻¹ μ-integrable as in thm:supp-memory-RG); (5) minimal positive-memory dilation rank cannot increase: formalised as the SOURCE RANK rank μ((0,∞)) = rank B_μ^* of the minimal dilation (IsMinimalRealization.finrank_range_eq shows it is the rank of the source map V = B^* of every minimal realization, including the canonical K_μ), monotone under compression (sourceRank_compress_le) and cutoff (sourceRank_cutoff_le) — this is what the paper's proof sentence 'compression of a positive minimal memory dilation carrier cannot increase source rank' asserts; the image head function is W^* F_{RG_Λ(A,μ)} W (headFunction_image, using dynamicFunction_compress for isometric W); (6) monodromy descends exactly when ker q_* ≤ ker ρ (monodromy_descends_iff). New infrastructure: PositiveTailMeasure.compress (W^* μ W) with integral/self-energy/head-correction/cutoff commutation (Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureCompressionExact.lean). |
| `lem:supp-positive-decomposition` | lemma | Positive $d^2$-direction decomposition | [`RenewalGeometry.stencilDecomposition`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PositiveStencilDecompositionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.stencilDecomposition_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PositiveStencilDecompositionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.stencilDiagonalWeight_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PositiveStencilDecompositionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.stencilPlusWeight_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PositiveStencilDecompositionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.stencilMinusWeight_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PositiveStencilDecompositionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.positive_stencil_decomposition`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/PositiveStencilDecompositionExact.lean) | Index type is any finite linearly ordered type ι (Fin d in the paper), d = Fintype.card ι, matrices over ℝ. Hypotheses: A symmetric (Matrix.IsSymm), η > 0, \|A i j\| < η for i ≠ j, (d-1)η < A i i. positive_stencil_decomposition gives strict positivity of w_i = A i i - (d-1)η, w⁺_ij = (η + A i j)/2, w⁻_ij = (η - A i j)/2 (the latter two for i ≠ j) and the identity A = Σ_i w_i e_i e_iᵀ + Σ_i Σ_j stencilPairTerm i j, where stencilPairTerm i j = w⁺_ij (e_i+e_j)(e_i+e_j)ᵀ + w⁻_ij (e_i-e_j)(e_i-e_j)ᵀ for i < j and 0 otherwise (so the double sum is exactly the paper's Σ_{i<j}). The identity stencilDecomposition_eq is proved from symmetry alone; positivity uses the window. This is the existence half complementing the psd/trace consistency in RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/IntegerStencil.lean. |
| `thm:supp-general-renewal-process` | theorem | Reversible coframe Renewal process |
