DirichletFormLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeRenewalPacket.sum_torus_add_latticeStep`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CoframeDirichletFormLimitExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.UnitCubeRiemannSum.tendsto_riemannSum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/UnitCubeRiemannSum.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LineTaylorRemainderBound.symmetric_product_expansion_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LineTaylorRemainderBound.lean) | All five clauses proved on the chart / torus. Clauses (1)-(4) as before (CoframeRenewalPacketExact: detailed balance, exact bracket eq:supp-general-bracket, bracket error eq:supp-general-bracket-error entrywise O(h^2), generator consistency eq:supp-general-generator with explicit constant linear in the C^4 bounds of f, for 0 < h ≤ 1, d ≥ 2, ρ ≠ 0). Clause (5), eq:supp-general-form-limit (new, CoframeDirichletFormLimitExact): the packet is placed on the torus Q_h = hℤ^d/ℤ^d, h = 1/M, vertices k/M with k ∈ (ℤ/Mℤ)^d, for ℤ^d-periodic f, coefficients a_r and density ρ and integer lattice directions r (IntegerDirections; the paper's R_d^+ ⊂ ℤ^d). dirichletForm_eq_energySum is the exact summation by parts −⟨f, L_h f⟩_{m_h} = Σ_x Σ_r c_h(x,r)\|f(x+hr) − f(x)\|² (torus translation invariance sum_torus_add_latticeStep via the bijection k ↦ k + r on (ℤ/Mℤ)^d); abs_energySum_sub_riemannSum_le shows the finite energy equals the Riemann sum h^d Σ_x ½Σ_r a_r(x)(∂_r f(x))² up to h·Σ_r K_r (K_r explicit from the C^2 bounds F1, F2 of f and the C^0/C^1 bounds A0, A1 of a_r); tendsto_dirichletForm gives −⟨f, L_h f⟩_{m_h} → ∫_{[0,1]^d} ½ Σ_r a_r (∂_r f)² dx as h = 1/(n+1) → 0, and tendsto_dirichletForm_metric rewrites the limit as ½∫ g^{ij} ∂_i f ∂_j f ρ dx under the positive decomposition ρ g^{-1} = Σ_r a_r r rᵀ (energyDensity_eq_metricDensity). Reusable infrastructure: Analysis/UnitCubeRiemannSum.lean (left-endpoint Riemann sums of continuous functions on the unit cube converge to the Lebesgue integral: tendsto_riemannSum, cell decomposition of [0,1)^d). Scoped hypotheses disclosed: f real-valued (∂_j \bar f = ∂_j f), f ∈ C² with global derivative bounds (the paper's smooth periodic extension), coefficients C¹ with global bounds and periodic, density periodic and nonvanishing, 2 ≤ d (the conductance exponent h^{d−2}), integer directions; the paper's uniform convergence is stated as convergence along the mesh sequence h = 1/(n+1). |

pinMarking.antiperiodic`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactSpinMarking.holonomy_antiperiodic`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactSpinMarking.not_gauge_equiv_antiperiodic`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactSpinMarking.exists_gauge_trivial_on_arc`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactSpinMarking.ArcLocal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactSpinMarking.GaugeInvariant`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactSpinMarking.local_invariant_const`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompactSpinMarking.spinorBracket_sign`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactSpinMarkingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeRenewalPacket.CoframePacket.abs_bracket_sub_inverseMetric_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CoframeRenewalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeRenewalPacket.CoframePacket.inverseMetric_eq_of_decomposition`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CoframeRenewalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.frameConnection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.spinorConnection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.spinConnectionField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeRenewalPacket.coframeInverseMetric_rotate`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CoframeRenewalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeRenewalPacket.packet_of_metric_rotate`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CoframeRenewalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeRenewalPacket.exists_distinct_coframes_same_metric`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CoframeRenewalPacketExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CoframeRenewalPacket.planeFlip_mem_specialOrthogonalGroup`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CoframeRenewalPacketExact.lean) | Proved; assembled in metric_does_not_select_spin_marking (+ spinLift_nonvacuous). (i) The bracket determines g^{-1}: CoframePacket.abs_bracket_sub_inverseMetric_le / inverseMetric_eq_of_decomposition (existing). (ii) Not the oriented orthonormal coframe: exists_distinct_coframes_same_metric (existing; identity vs an SO(d) rotation, same g^{-1}). (iii) Smooth rotation R(x) in SO(d) on an open chart (E -> R E, e -> e R^T): g^{-1}, g, dg, the Levi-Civita Christoffel symbols and rho = det e are unchanged (ginv_rotate[_field], metric_rotate, metricDeriv_rotate using dR^T R + R^T dR = 0 derived from R^T R = 1 on the chart by partialMat_orthogonal, christoffel_rotate, density_rotate), and a CoframePacket decomposes rho g^{-1} for E iff it does for R E (isDecomposition_rotate_iff), so the bracket and the scalar Renewal generator are literally the same; the Levi-Civita frame connection obeys the gauge law omega' = R omega R^T + R (dR)^T = R omega R^{-1} + R dR^{-1} (frameConnection_rotate; field version spinConnectionField_rotate with the actual derivative of R(y), via the product rule partialMat_mul) and the induced spinor connection is Omega' = 1/4 (R omega R^T + R dR^T)_ab gamma_a gamma_b (spinorConnection_rotate); frameConnection_rotate_changes exhibits a jet where omega = 0 but omega' != 0 (the connection really changes). (iv) Not a spin lift: IsSpinLift gamma S R (S invertible, S gamma_b S^{-1} = R_ab gamma_a: the Clifford-module realisation of the central extension Spin(d) -> SO(d) with kernel {+-1}); IsSpinLift.neg + spinLift_ne_neg: -S != S lifts the same R (same coframe, same g^{-1}); IsSpinLift.conj_spinor: the homogeneous part of the spinor connection transforms by conjugation with a lift; non-vacuity in d = 2 with Pauli generators: S_theta = cos(theta/2) - sin(theta/2) gamma_1 gamma_2 lifts R_theta in SO(2) (isSpinLift_spinRot), S_{theta+2pi} = -S_theta while R_{theta+2pi} = R_theta (spinRot_add_two_pi, rot2_add_two_pi): the closed frame loop has spin holonomy -1 (spinRot_two_pi). (v) Not a global spin structure, and Z_2 holonomy invisible to local brackets: spin markings on the n-cycle as Z_2 edge cochains modulo vertex gauge (SpinCochain, gauge); the holonomy is gauge invariant (holonomy_gauge), the antiperiodic marking has holonomy -1 so it is not gauge equivalent to the periodic one (not_gauge_equiv_antiperiodic), but on the contractible arc obtained by deleting one edge every marking is gauge trivial (exists_gauge_trivial_on_arc), hence every gauge-invariant arc-local functional takes the same value on all markings (local_invariant_const); quadratic transport brackets (s U)^H M (s U) are sign-blind (spinorBracket_sign). Renderings disclosed: global spin structures are modelled by their relative Z_2 marking on a cycle graph (the torsor under H^1(.;Z_2) of the plan) rather than by spin bundles over a manifold; 'every local metric bracket' is rendered as every gauge-invariant functional reading only a contractible arc; spin lifts are Clifford-module lifts; the inhomogeneous spinor term is written 1/4 (R dR^T)_ab gamma_a gamma_b (its identification with S dS^{-1} for a differentiable lift is not formalised). |
| `lem:supp-density-symmetric` | lemma | Exact density-symmetric Dirac identity | [`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.densitySymmetric_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.geometricDirac`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.cliffordField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.spinConnectionField`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.halfDensity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.density`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.density_eq_sqrt_det_metric`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.partialMat_frame_coframe`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.hasDerivAt_det_coframe`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.hasDerivAt_inv_halfDensity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.densitySymmetric_pointwise`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.contracted_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.metric_compatibility`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.sum_christoffel_trace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.spinorConnection_commutator`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.frameConnection_antisymm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.metric_mul_christoffel_add`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.christoffel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.frameConnection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.spinorConnection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.cliffordCoeff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.cliffordCoeff_anticomm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.IsClifford`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricSpinDirac.densitySymmetric_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/DensitySymmetricSpinDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricDirac.CliffordModuleDerivationData.densitySymmetric_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/DensitySymmetricDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricDirac.CliffordModuleDerivationData.conj_sum_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/DensitySymmetricDiracExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DensitySymmetricDirac.CliffordModuleDerivationData`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/DensitySymmetricDiracExact.lean) | Proved for the actual geometric data; the contracted identity is now DERIVED, not stored (checklist b). Data on an open chart Q (checklist h: open chart, not whole space): frame E(y) = (E_a^j) and coframe e(y) with E e = 1 on Q, entrywise differentiable at x in Q, oriented det e(x) > 0, Clifford matrices gamma_a (IsClifford: gamma_a gamma_b + gamma_b gamma_a = 2 delta_ab). Everything else is defined from the data: g^{-1} = E^T E, g = e e^T, the Levi-Civita Christoffel symbols christoffel (1/2 g^{kn}(d_j g_nl + d_l g_nj - d_n g_jl)), the Levi-Civita frame connection omega_jab = e^a_m(d_j E_b^m + Gamma^m_jl E_b^l) and spinor connection Omega_j = 1/4 omega_jab gamma_a gamma_b, c^k = E_a^k gamma_a, rho = det e = sqrt(det g) (density_eq_sqrt_det_metric). Proved chain, exactly the paper's proof: cliffordCoeff_anticomm (eq:supp-clifford-coeff, c^j c^k + c^k c^j = 2 g^jk I); metric_mul_christoffel_add (nabla g = 0) and partialMat_frame_coframe (d(E e) = 0 on the chart) give frameConnection_antisymm; spinorConnection_commutator ([Omega_j, gamma_c] = omega_jac gamma_a); metric_compatibility (d_j c^k + [Omega_j, c^k] + Gamma^k_jl c^l = 0, the first display of the proof); sum_christoffel_trace (Gamma^j_jl = tr(E d_l e)) with hasDerivAt_det_coframe (Jacobi via RenewalGeometry.hasDerivAt_det: d_l det e = det e tr(E d_l e), i.e. Gamma^j_jl = d_l log rho); contracted_identity; and the operator identity densitySymmetric_identity (eq:supp-density-symmetric): rho^{1/2}(x) D_g(rho^{-1/2} psi)(x) = -(i/2) sum_j (c^j nabla_j psi + nabla_j(c^j psi))(x) for every spinor field psi differentiable at x, with D_g = -i sum c^j(d_j + Omega_j) (geometricDirac, eq:supp-geometric-dirac) and the right side the continuum operator CovariantWilsonCoreConsistency.densitySymmetricDirac used in lem:supp-general-core. Non-vacuity (checklist k): densitySymmetric_nonvacuous instantiates all hypotheses for the non-constant conformal coframe e = exp(y_0^2) I on R^2 (metric exp(2 y_0^2) delta, curvature -2 exp(-2 y_0^2), non-flat) with Pauli generators. Rendering disclosed: the identity is the pointwise identity of differential operators (on spinor fields differentiable at the point), which is what eq:supp-density-symmetric asserts on L^2(Q, S_d; rho dx) for smooth sections; partial derivatives are lineDeriv along coordinate directions; the coframe e is given together with E (e = E^{-1} on Q) and assumed differentiable. The earlier abstract surrogate (DensitySymmetricDirac.CliffordModuleDerivationData with the contracted identity as a field) is kept but no longer needed. |

metry.CovariantWilsonGarding.covariantGardingConstant`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.covariant_garding`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.covariant_garding_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.covariant_garding_nonvacuous`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.gridInner_covShift`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.covVarWilson_symmetric`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.covVarWilson_matMul`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.dbl`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.doubledCoeff`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.doubledNormal`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.doubledGrading`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.doubledLinks`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.doubled_cliffordData`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.doubled_grading`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonGarding.doubled_symmetric`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonGardingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinAtlasRealGrading.AtlasRealGrading`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinAtlasRealGradingExact.lean) | Proved. Frozen symbol eq:supp-general-frozen-symbol (frozenWilson_planeWave), square eq:supp-general-frozen-square (frozenSymbol_sq), unique zero theta=0 on the Brillouin torus (frozenSymbol_eq_zero_iff), eq:frozen-uniform-ellipticity (frozenSymbolScalar_bounds): unchanged. NEW (the audited gaps): (a) the graph estimate eq:supp-general-Garding now holds for the covariant operator of eq:supp-general-Wilson WITH spin links, covVarWilson = 1/2 sum_j (M_c P^Omega_j + P^Omega_j M_c) + varpi Gamma W^Omega, T^Omega_j u(x) = U_j(x) u(x+e_j), (T^Omega_j)^* u(x) = U_j(x-e_j)^H u(x-e_j) (same definitions as CovariantWilsonCoreConsistency.covShift/covShiftAdj, on the torus). From the paper's link hypothesis \|\|U_j(x) - I\|\| <= C_U h (LinkBound; the adjoint-link bound is derived by euclNormSq_conjTranspose_mulVec_le, and linkBound_of_connection derives LinkBound from U = I + h Omega + O(h^2) with bounded connection, the paper's 'T^Omega - S = O(h) in a smooth spin frame'), norm_eucl_linkDifference_le gives \|\|(D^Omega - D^S)u\|\| <= d C_U (B + \|varpi\| G) \|\|u\|\| uniformly in h, and covariant_garding / covariant_garding_norm give \|\|u\|\|_{1,h}^2 <= C(\|\|D^Omega u\|\|_h^2 + \|\|u\|\|_h^2) and \|\|u\|\|_{1,h} <= sqrt C (\|\|D^Omega u\|\|_h + \|\|u\|\|_h) with the explicit h- and n-independent constant covariantGardingConstant = gardingConstant * (2 + 2 (d C_U (B + \|varpi\| G))^2). covariant_garding_nonvacuous discharges every hypothesis for a nontrivial instance (doubled 1 (x) sigma_1 coefficient, Gamma = 1 (x) sigma_2, non-identity links (1+ih)I). (b) Self-adjointness of the covariant operator: covVarWilson_symmetric (Hermitian coefficients, Hermitian Gamma commuting with the links; T^Omega adjointness gridInner_covShift). (c) Grading: covVarWilson_matMul (a Hermitian gamma anticommuting with c^j(x), Gamma and commuting with links anticommutes with D^Omega) and, for the paper's fixed doubled convention eq:supp-doubled-clifford c-hat^j = c^j (x) sigma_1, Gamma_perp = I (x) sigma_2, gamma-hat = I (x) sigma_3, links U (x) I (dbl via Kronecker product reindexed to Fin (M*2)), doubled_grading derives gamma-hat D^Omega = -D^Omega gamma-hat for arbitrary undoubled coefficients and links, doubled_symmetric the self-adjointness, and doubled_cliffordData derives the DoubledCliffordData hypothesis of the frozen square from the undoubled Clifford relations eq:supp-clifford-coeff. The real-structure clause is conditional in the statement ('retained when its compatibility is supplied as in Definition def:stable-spin-atlas'); the compatibility data is StableSpinAtlasRealGrading.AtlasRealGrading. Scoped renderings (unchanged from the previous pass, disclosed): the periodic coordinate box is the lattice torus (Z/nZ)^d and every torus section is admissible; the density-conjugated l^2 norm is the cell-weighted grid norm with the density absorbed into the sampled coefficients; the partition of unity, cutoff-gradient bound K, Lipschitz bound L and modulus of continuity omega are explicit hypotheses with the absorption condition 4 d omega^2 C_0 <= 1 (the paper's 'choose r once'). |

ysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.norm_coeff_taylor_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.frozenSym`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.CoeffBounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.sobolevSqH2`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.sobolevSqH3`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.coreL2Constant`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.transSample`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreL2.stencilForm`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyL2.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.cellAvg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.cellIdx`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.cell`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.lintegral_cellAvg_sub_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.lintegral_sq_lintegral_add_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.lintegral_sq_ballAverage_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.lintegral_cell_shift_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.enorm_taylor_one_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LatticeCellL2.enorm_sub_le_lintegral_fderiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/LatticeCellL2Estimates.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactSpinTransport.IsExactInverseTransport`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/ExactSpinTransportLinks.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactSpinTransport.linkExpansion_of_exactTransport`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/ExactSpinTransportLinks.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreConsistency.covariantWilson`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreConsistency.densitySymmetricDirac`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreConsistency.LinkExpansion`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CovariantWilsonCoreConsistency.ConnectionBounds`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/CovariantWilsonCoreConsistencyExact.lean) | Proved (L^2/Sobolev form with cell-average sampling, the three audited gaps closed). Setting: covariant doubled Wilson operator covariantWilson h varpi c U Gamma = 1/2 sum_j (M_{c^j(hx)} P^Omega_j + P^Omega_j M_{c^j(hx)}) + varpi Gamma W^Omega (eq:supp-general-Wilson, links U, general d and fibre C^N), applied to the CELL AVERAGES S_h psi = cellAvg h psi (x -> h^{-d} int_{cell x} psi, = (J_h^0)^* for the cell weight h^d) and reconstructed piecewise constantly (J_h^0 u (y) = u(cellIdx h y); lintegral_comp_cellIdx: int \|u(cellIdx y)\|^2 = h^d sum_x \|u x\|^2, i.e. J_h^0 is isometric for \|\|.\|\|_h). (1) eq:supp-general-core: lintegral_covariantWilson_cellAvg_sub_densitySymmetricDirac_le gives \|\|J_h^0 D~ S_h psi - (-(i/2) sum_j (c^j nabla_j + nabla_j c^j)) psi\|\|_{L^2}^2 <= K h^2 \|\|psi\|\|_{H^2}^2 for every C^2 psi and 0 < h <= 1, with K = coreL2Constant (explicit, depending only on d and the bounds C0, C1, C2 of c^j, of its e_j-derivative c'^j and of the Lipschitz constant of c'^j, the connection bounds B0, B1, the link constant C_U and varpi, \|\|Gamma\|\|); sobolevSqH2 psi = sum_{k<=2} int \|\|D^k psi\|\|^2 (operator norms of the Frechet derivatives, an equivalent H^2 norm), and sobolevSqH2 <= sobolevSqH3 gives the paper's \|\|psi\|\|_{H^3}. The proof follows the paper: covariantWilson_cellAvg (exact: the operator on cell averages is the cell average of the operator on translated point samples), Taylor with integral remainder along lattice segments (LatticeCellL2Estimates.enorm_taylor_one_le), the frozen-coefficient decomposition with the discrete Leibniz rule for P^Omega(c psi) (no bounds on the products c^j psi are assumed any more: the previous M'_k hypotheses are gone; densitySymmetricDirac_eq_frozenSym derives the Leibniz rule from CoeffBounds), domination of cell integrals by local averages over balls of radius 2h, the L^2 boundedness of translation kernels (Young/Minkowski), and the cell Poincare inequality \|\|J S f - f\|\|^2 <= 2^d h^2 \|\|Df\|\|^2 (lintegral_cellAvg_sub_sq_le). (2) eq:Wilson-core-smallness: wilsonCore_smallness h^d sum_x \|\|W^Omega_h S_h psi (x)\|\|^2 <= K h^2 \|\|psi\|\|_{H^2}^2. (3) Identification with U_rho D-hat_g U_rho^{-1}: lintegral_covariantWilson_cellAvg_sub_geometricDirac_le composes with the proved lem:supp-density-symmetric (densitySymmetric_identity) for c^j = E_a^j gamma_a and the Levi-Civita spin connection of the frame, and supp_general_core_exactTransport states both clauses for EXACT inverse spin parallel transport links (IsExactInverseTransport, LinkExpansion derived by linkExpansion_of_exactTransport) with \|\|psi\|\|_{H^3} in the first clause. Scoped renderings (disclosed): the estimate is on the whole coordinate space R^d with lattice hZ^d and coefficients/frame defined and bounded on R^d (the paper's smooth periodic extension is such a choice); for psi supported in the interior of the periodic box and small h the periodic and whole-space quantities coincide, so the statement covers the paper's (and is stronger: no support restriction); fibres C^N carry the sup norm (equivalent to the Euclidean norm up to sqrt N); the coefficient regularity is C^{1,1} along e_j (bounded, Lipschitz c^j with bounded, Lipschitz e_j-derivative), which the smooth coefficients of the paper satisfy. Non-vacuity: the example after supp_general_core_exactTransport instantiates every hypothesis (flat c^j = 1, trivial transport, Gamma = 1); ExactSpinTransportLinks has the nontrivial exact-transport example Omega = i I. All new declarations use only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |

try/OperatorLimits/VariableTorusDirac.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VariableTorusDirac.covPlaneSymbol`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/VariableTorusDirac.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VariableTorusDirac.covVarWilson_planeWave`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/VariableTorusDirac.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VariableTorusDirac.covStage_adjoint_modeVec`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/VariableTorusDirac.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VariableTorusDirac.cosCoeffs`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/VariableTorusDirac.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VariableTorusDirac.cosCoeffs_c_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/VariableTorusDirac.lean) | PROVED (all clauses). (1) eq:supp-global-norm-resolvent and the spectral-projection clause for a compatible stable spin discretization (abstract surrogate CompatibleStableAtlas of def:stable-spin-atlas, unchanged: CompatibleStableAtlas.tendsto_norm_embeddedResolvent_sub, tendsto_norm_embeddedSpectralProjection_sub with genuine spectral projections 1_(a,b)); (2) retention of compatible real/grading structures (AtlasRealGrading.tendsto_embed_stageReal_adjoint / tendsto_embed_stageGrading_adjoint, unchanged); (3) NEW (h7): eq:supp-local-norm-resolvent and the spectral-projection clause on the fixed smooth periodic coordinate extension with GENUINELY VARIABLE coefficients, VariableTorusDirac.Coeffs.supp_local_norm_resolvent; all clauses assembled in StableSpinNormResolvent.supp_compact_spin_resolvent. Data of (3), all the paper's: Coeffs C on T^d (continuous Hermitian doubled Clifford coefficients c^j differentiable along e_j with continuous d_j c^j, continuous skew-Hermitian connection Omega_j; weaker than smooth); Gamma_perp Hermitian commuting with Omega (automatic in the doubled convention Omega (x) I, I (x) sigma_2); uniform ellipticity of the doubled data at every point, UniformlyElliptic (c^j(y)) Gamma lam Lam (Clifford relations c^j c^k + c^k c^j = 2 g^{jk}(y) I, Gamma c^j + c^j Gamma = 0, Gamma^2 = 1, lam\|xi\|^2 <= g(y)(xi,xi) <= Lam\|xi\|^2: the paper's 'extend the coefficients smoothly to a fixed periodic coordinate box, preserving uniform ellipticity'); varpi != 0 (paper: varpi > 0); links U_n that are exact inverse spin parallel transport of Omega along each lattice edge (Coeffs.IsTransportLinks: U_j(x) = Phi(h), Phi' = Phi Omega_j(x/m + t e_j), Phi(0) = I, as in eq:supp-covariant-differences). D^W_{g,h} = covStage (covVarWilson with sampled c^j(x/m), eq:supp-general-Wilson), W_h = J^0_h (isometric piecewise-constant embedding), S_h = (J^0_h)^*. Conclusions: every stage operator is self-adjoint and stageRes is its resolvent; D-hat_g, the self-adjoint realisation of -(i/2) sum_j (c^j nabla_j + nabla_j c^j), has domain H^1 and acts as C.op there (constructed from the discrete estimates, never assumed); \|\|J (D_h - z)^{-1} J^* - (D-hat_g - z)^{-1}\|\| -> 0 for every non-real z; spectral projections of windows (a,b) with non-eigenvalue endpoints converge in norm. The two discrete hypotheses of covariantDiscretizationOfGarding are now PROVED for variable coefficients: (A2) Coeffs.exists_uniform_garding gives ONE constant G for all meshes h = 1/(n+1), via covariant_garding with a sampled Lipschitz square partition of unity of fixed patch size 1/M chosen once from the modulus of continuity of c (new infrastructure Analysis/TorusSquarePartitionOfUnity.lean: TorusPartition.bump with sum_sq_bump = 1, support bound bump_ne_zero, Lipschitz bound abs_bump_add_lineVec_sub_le), frozen points = lattice points nearest to the patch centres, the absorption threshold 4 d omega^2 frozenConstant <= 1 enforced, the Lipschitz bound from d_j c^j (mean value), the link bound C_U h from exact transport (Gronwall), Gamma commuting with the links derived from Gamma commuting with Omega (transport_comm), coarse meshes by h1NormSq_le_coarse; (A3) Coeffs.tendsto_covStage_planeWave: J D_h J^* (e^{2 pi i k.x} v) -> A_k e^{2 pi i k.x} v = D-hat_g(e^{2 pi i k.x} v) in L^2, from the exact first-order algebra of the plane-wave symbol (covPlaneSymbol_eq, Coeffs.symbField_eq), the uniform convergence sup_x \|\|covPlaneSymbol(x) - A_k(x/m)\|\| -> 0 (Coeffs.uSmall_covPlaneSymbol_sub) built from the o(h) transport expansion for a merely continuous connection (norm_transport_sub_le_of_osc, Coeffs.uSmall_link), the uniform C^1 Taylor expansion of c^j along e_j (Coeffs.norm_entry_taylor_le, uSmall_diffQuot) and e^{2 pi i k_j/m} = 1 + 2 pi i k_j/m + O(m^-2), followed by the cellwise sup estimate for the piecewise-constant embedding (coeFn_pcEmbedding, mFourier_sub_sample_le, uniform continuity of A_k, tendsto_modeFactor). Non-vacuity: cosDoubledCoeffs on T^1 with c^1(x) = (3 + 2 cos 2 pi x) sigma_1 (genuinely variable: 5 sigma_1 at x = 0; g(x) in [1,25]), Gamma_perp = sigma_2, NON-TRIVIAL connection Omega = i I with its exact transport links exp(i h I) (cosDoubledCoeffs_isTransportLinks), varpi = 1: every hypothesis is discharged (example + cosDoubled_tendsto_norm_resolvent). Rendering notes: the periodic extension is encoded directly in the density-symmetric form on L^2(T^d; C^K) (lem:supp-density-symmetric; C.op is identified with -(i/2) sum_j (c^j nabla_j + nabla_j c^j) on plane waves in VariableTorusDirac); existence of exact transport links for a general continuous connection (a linear matrix ODE) is not re-proved, the links are input data characterised by their defining ODE; the global clause is stated for the abstract CompatibleStableAtlas form of def:stable-spin-atlas. Paper notation (undoubled data): Coeffs.doubled builds c^j (x) sigma_1, d_j c^j (x) sigma_1, Omega (x) I from undoubled periodic coefficients; Coeffs.doubled_uniformlyElliptic DERIVES the uniform-ellipticity hypothesis from c^j c^k + c^k c^j = 2 g^{jk}(y) I and lam\|xi\|^2 <= g(y)(xi,xi) <= Lam\|xi\|^2; Coeffs.doubled_normal_comm derives [Gamma_perp, Omega-hat] = 0; Coeffs.supp_local_norm_resolvent_doubled states the local clause in the paper's notation (non-vacuity: the undoubled cosCoeffs, c(x) = 3 + 2 cos 2 pi x, g = c^2); Coeffs.doubled_grading: gamma-hat = I (x) sigma_3 anticommutes with the covariant Wilson operator with exact transport links (grading supplied by the fixed doubled convention). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. |
| `cor:supp-flat-torus-spin` | corollary | Unconditional periodic flat-spin norm-resolvent convergence | tidim.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCellEmbedding.pcEmbedding_adjoint_mode`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusCellEmbeddingMultidim.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCellEmbedding.tendsto_modeFactor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusCellEmbeddingMultidim.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCellEmbedding.inner_mode_pcEmbedding_modeSample_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusCellEmbeddingMultidim.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TorusCellEmbedding.norm_sum_modeError_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Continuum/TorusCellEmbeddingMultidim.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HilbertBasisDiagonal.data`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/HilbertBasisDiagonalSelfAdjoint.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HilbertBasisDiagonal.core_dense`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/HilbertBasisDiagonalSelfAdjoint.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HilbertBasisDiagonal.isCompactOperator_mulCLM`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/HilbertBasisDiagonalSelfAdjoint.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompatibleStableAtlas.tendsto_norm_embeddedResolvent_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompatibleStableAtlasNormResolvent.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FlatTorusSpinAtlas.hasDerivAt_mFourier_line`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/FlatTorusSpinAtlas.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FlatTorusSpinAtlas.sum_dirac_differential`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/FlatTorusSpinAtlas.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SummarySpin.flatTorusSpin_norm_resolvent_ofFrame`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SummarySpinExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SummarySpin.ginv_posDef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/SummarySpinExact.lean) | Proved: flatTorusSpin_norm_resolvent. Data as in the paper: torus R^d/Z^d with a constant positive definite inverse metric g, periodic (trivial) spin structure, constant Hermitian Clifford coefficients c^j on C^M with c^j c^k + c^k c^j = 2 g^{jk}, doubled convention c-hat^j = c^j (x) sigma_1, Gamma_perp = I (x) sigma_2 (ofUndoubled derives the doubled Clifford data and the ellipticity constants from g.PosDef), trivial spin transport, uniform lattice h = 1/(n+1), the Wilson operator eq:supp-general-Wilson with constant coefficients (stage = FrozenWilsonGarding.frozenWilson on the lattice torus), fixed varpi > 0. Conclusion: \|\|J_h (D^W_{g,h} - z)^{-1} J_h^* - (D-hat - z)^{-1}\|\| -> 0 for every non-real z, with (D^W - z)^{-1} = Ring.inverse (D^W - z), J_h the isometric piecewise-constant embedding (TorusCellEmbedding.pcEmbedding, componentwise) and D-hat = dirac: the self-adjoint operator (resolvent data) diagonal in the Hilbert basis e^{2 pi i k.x} u_i(k) of eigenvectors of the Hermitian symbol sum_j 2 pi k_j c-hat^j, which acts on plane waves as sum_j c-hat^j(-i d_j) (dirac_modeVec, flatTorusSpin_dirac_modeVec) and has the trigonometric polynomials as a core; and sum_dirac_differential with hasDerivAt_mFourier_line show that on e^{2 pi i k.y} v the differential operator sum_j c-hat^j(-i d_j) gives e^{2 pi i k.y} sigma(k) v pointwise; hence dirac is the closure of the doubled Dirac operator on smooth periodic spinors (the trigonometric polynomials are a core). flatTorusSpin_norm_resolvent_ofFrame derives the hypotheses from the paper's data c^j = E_a^j gamma_a of a constant invertible frame with Hermitian Clifford matrices gamma_a (g = E^T E is positive definite, ginv_posDef). 'Thus the compatible-stability hypotheses are automatically satisfied': atlas is an instance of CompatibleStableAtlas with every field derived: (A1) J J^* -> I strongly (best approximation + density of modes), reconstruction I_h = trigonometric interpolation into H^1 with \|\|I_h u\|\|_{H^1} <= \|\|u\|\|_{1,h} and \|\|I_h u - J_h u\|\| <= (pi sqrt d / 2m) \|\|u\|\|_{1,h} (aliasing orthogonality of the interpolation errors, proved via the translation structure of the cells); (A2) from the constant-coefficient Fourier Garding bound h1NormSq_le; (A3) with the paper's samples S_h = (J_h^0)^*: J^* of a plane wave is gamma_h times a lattice plane wave (pcEmbedding_adjoint_mode), D_h acts on it by the frozen symbol, the frozen symbol tends to the continuum symbol (tendsto_frozenSymbol), extended to all trigonometric polynomials by span induction. Rellich: the H^1 embedding is a compact diagonal multiplier. Renderings disclosed: (1) L^2(T^d; C^K) is realised componentwise as the l^2-direct sum of K copies of L^2(T^d) (Haar probability measure); (2) H^1 is realised in Fourier coordinates as l^2 with the weight (1+\|k\|^2)^{1/2}; (3) the reconstruction I_h used in the proof is the band-limited trigonometric interpolant instead of the paper's piecewise-affine one (the corollary's statement does not involve I_h; (A1) holds for it with explicit constants); (4) the stage space is the unweighted l^2 with physical section u = m^{d/2} v, so that J_h is the paper's isometric piecewise-constant map and \|\|u\|\|_h = \|\|v\|\|. Non-vacuity: the example in section nonvacuity instantiates the theorem on the flat two-torus with the Pauli coefficients (K = 4, varpi = 1, z = i). The proof uses the proved theorem CompatibleStableAtlas.tendsto_norm_embeddedResolvent_sub (eq:supp-global-norm-resolvent), not an open record's conclusion as a hypothesis. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. |
| `cor:supp-compact-spin-image` | corollary | Conditional stable compact-spin approximation | [`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.compactSpinImage_riemannian`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.compactSpinImage_riemannian_marked`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.IsMarkedStableSpinImage`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.AdmitsMarkedStableSpinDiscretization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.isMarkedStableSpinImage_of_admits`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.isMarkedStableSpinImage_of_admits_of_connectedSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.isMarkedStableSpinImage_forall`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.inner_antiunitary_map_map`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.embedAdjoint_grading`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.embedAdjoint_realJ`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.stageGrading_stageResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.stageReal_stageResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.grading_embeddedResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.realJ_embeddedResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.grading_limit_resolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.realJ_limit_resolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinRiemannianImage.admitsMarkedStableSpinDiscretization_example`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.ApproxMeshSequence.global_metric_riemannian`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphRiemannian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.MeshGraphDistanceConvergence.exists_approxMeshSequence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/MeshGraphRiemannian.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CompatibleStableAtlas.tendsto_norm_embeddedResolvent_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/CompatibleStableAtlasNormResolvent.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StableSpinAtlasRealGrading.AtlasRealGrading`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinAtlasRealGradingExact.lean) | Proved (new file Spectralization/StableSpinRiemannianImageExact.lean). The corollary is restated as the paper's statement: for every closed Riemannian manifold M (Mathlib: compact, boundaryless model, IsManifold I 1, continuous RiemannianBundle, IsRiemannianManifold so d_g = Riemannian distance; MetricSpace version = finite distance, plus the EMetricSpace+ConnectedSpace version), every compatible stable spin discretization A with hypotheses exactly those of def:stable-spin-atlas (CompatibleStableAtlas (A1)-(A3); for the marks AtlasRealGrading) and every refining mesh sequence S satisfying the mesh assumptions of thm:supp-global-metric (ApproxMeshSequence: h-nets, h/rho->0, rho->0, all pairs within rho joined, \|l/d_g-1\|<=eps->0). compactSpinImage_riemannian: (1) \|\|W_h(D_h-z)^-1W_h^* - (D-hat - z)^-1\|\| -> 0 for every non-real z (generalized norm resolvent); (2) compact limit resolvent; (3) \|\|[D_h^met,pi_h(g)]\|\| = max_e \|g(x_e)-g(y_e)\|/l_e; (4) eventually Connes distance = graph metric; (5) an error sequence -> 0 eventually bounds \|d_Connes,h(x,y) - d_g(x,y)\| on all vertex pairs (convergent metric heads, from ApproxMeshSequence.global_metric_riemannian, no chain surrogate); (6) L_h^met(f\|V_h) -> sup_p \|\|df_p\|\| for C^1 f. compactSpinImage_riemannian_marked adds the retained marks, all DERIVED from AtlasRealGrading (new infrastructure): KO relations J_h^2=eps, gamma_h^2=1, J_h gamma_h = eps'' gamma_h J_h, gamma_h self-adjoint; W_h J_h W_h^* -> J, W_h gamma_h W_h^* -> gamma strongly; exact stage identities gamma R_h(z) = -R_h(-z) gamma and J R_h(z) = eps' R_h(eps' conj z) J for the compressed resolvents (via W_h^* gamma = gamma_h W_h^*, W_h^* J = J_h W_h^*, <Jx,Jy> = <y,x> for antiunitaries), and the same identities for (D-hat - z)^-1 by passing to the norm limit. Essential surjectivity: IsMarkedStableSpinImage M T J gamma eps' (the image: T is a generalized norm-resolvent limit of finite-dimensional marked stage triples D_h, W_h, J_h, gamma_h with the KO/intertwining relations, and finite metric heads on h-nets with h->0 whose Connes distances converge uniformly to d_g) is proved from AdmitsMarkedStableSpinDiscretization T J gamma eps' (existence of a CompatibleStableAtlas with limit T and an AtlasRealGrading with actions J, gamma, sign eps') in isMarkedStableSpinImage_of_admits (metric heads exist unconditionally by exists_approxMeshSequence); isMarkedStableSpinImage_forall: if discretizations are supplied for every target of a family, every target is in the image. Dependencies: only PROVED Lean results, no open conclusion is assumed: the global clause eq:supp-global-norm-resolvent of thm:supp-compact-spin-resolvent (CompatibleStableAtlas.tendsto_norm_embeddedResolvent_sub; that record is open only for its local periodic clause, the spectral-projection identification and retention, none of which the corollary uses except retention, proved here) and thm:supp-global-metric (proved). Disclosed renderings: as in the encoded def:stable-spin-atlas, the doubled spin Dirac operator D-hat on L^2(M;S(x)C^2) is the abstract surrogate (SelfAdjointResolventData on a Hilbert space H with core and compact first-order embedding); Mathlib has no spinor bundles, so H is not built from M; 'marked' = the asserted real/grading structures (def:marked-spectral-triple); the Connes-distance equality and convergence hold eventually/asymptotically (finitely many coarse meshes irrelevant). Non-vacuity (k): admitsMarkedStableSpinDiscretization_example (doubled one-point model C^2, D-hat = sigma_1, gamma = sigma_3 anticommuting, J = conjugation) and examples instantiating isMarkedStableSpinImage_of_admits and compactSpinImage_riemannian_marked on the closed Riemannian manifold EuclideanSpace R (Fin 0) (Mathlib has no compact Riemannian manifold of positive dimension). Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. |
| `thm:supp-Naimark-realization` | theorem | Operator-measure realization and minimality | [`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.naimark_realization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.stieltjesSelfEnergy_eq_compress`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.minimal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.toVectorMeasure_eq_compress`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.IsSpectralMeasure.ofSpectralMeasure`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.IsSpectralMeasure.stieltjesSelfEnergy_ofSpectralMeasure`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorMeasureL2.L2.topologicalClosure_sourceSpan`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OperatorMeasureL2Completion.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.IsMinimalRealization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkUniquenessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.PositiveTailMeasure.isMinimalRealization_canonical`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkUniquenessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.IsMinimalRealization.existsUnique_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkUniquenessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperatorTailMeasure.naimark_realization_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkUniquenessExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GramIsometry.existsUnique_linearIsometryEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/GramDeterminedIsometry.lean) | All four clauses proved. (1) eq:supp-Naimark-realization Σ_μ(z) = B_μ (C_μ−z)^{-1} B_μ^* for z ∉ [0,∞) (stieltjesSelfEnergy_eq_compress) and μ(E) = B_μ P_μ(E) B_μ^*; (2) eq:supp-Naimark-minimal (minimal); (3) the converse for a spectral measure P supported in (0,∞) (IsSpectralMeasure, the spectral measure E_C of the nonnegative self-adjoint C — Mathlib has no unbounded spectral theorem, so C is represented by its spectral measure and its resolvent by any operator acting as the spectral integral of (λ−z)^{-1} on source vectors) and B = V^*: ofSpectralMeasure, stieltjesSelfEnergy_ofSpectralMeasure; (4) eq:supp-Naimark-unitary, new in this pass: IsMinimalRealization μ P V bundles a spectral measure P on a Hilbert space K, ⟨Vξ, P(E) Vη⟩ = ⟨ξ, μ(E) η⟩ (μ(E) = B P(E) B^*) and K = closed span {P(E) V ξ}; the canonical (K_μ, P_μ, V_μ) is one (isMinimalRealization_canonical), and for any two minimal realizations there is a unique unitary U : K₁ ≃ₗᵢ K₂ with U P₁(E) = P₂(E) U for every Borel E (the spectral-measure form of U C₁ = C₂ U) and U V₁ = V₂ (= U B₁^* = B₂^*) (existsUnique_unitary, packaged with the canonical case in naimark_realization_unitary). Reusable infrastructure: GramIsometry.existsUnique_linearIsometryEquiv — generating families of two Hilbert spaces with equal Gram data are related by a unique unitary (Analysis/GramDeterminedIsometry.lean). |
| `thm:supp-complete-rational-Krein` | theorem | Complete normalized rational generalized-Nevanlinna realization | [`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.transfer_eq_laurent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.markov_adjoint`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.inner_nevanlinnaKernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.negIndex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.negIndex_pullback_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.negInertia_kernelGram_le_negIndex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.resolventSourceSpan_eq_top`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.negSquares`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.negSquares_transfer_eq_negIndex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PontryaginRationalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.canonical`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.canonical_markov`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.canonical_transfer_eq_toFun`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.canonical_isCyclic`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.finrank_carrier`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.mcMillanDegree_eq_finrank_columnSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.mcMillanDegree_le_realization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.canonical_form_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.negIndex_canonical_form`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.negSquares_canonical_transfer_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.negSquares_toFun_eq_negIndex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.existsUnique_sourceFixing_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.inverse_sub_algebraMap_eq_neg_tsum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ResolventNeumannSeries.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.resolvent_sub_resolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ResolventNeumannSeries.lean) | Proved for Q encoded as in def:supp-rational-Hermitian (Hermitian Laurent coefficients M_n at infinity with finite Hankel rank; H finite dimensional). A finite Pontryagin realization (PontryaginRealization) is a finite-dimensional Hilbert majorant N with an invertible self-adjoint Gram operator J (form [x,y] = <x,Jy>; a fundamental symmetry J^2 = 1 is the special case of a suitably normalised majorant, and the negative index does not depend on the majorant), a J-self-adjoint state operator A and a source Gamma; output Gamma^+ = Gamma^* J, Markov parameters M_n = Gamma^* J A^n Gamma (Hermitian: markov_adjoint), transfer function Q(z) = Gamma^* J (A-z)^{-1} Gamma. The canonical construction (Hankel state space on its Euclidean carrier, Hankel form as Gram operator, shift A_Q, source Gamma_Q) is NormalizedRationalHermitianData.canonical with: canonical_markov (L_Q A_Q^n Gamma_Q = M_n); canonical_transfer_eq_toFun = eq:supp-rational-realization, Q(z) = Gamma_Q^+ (A_Q - z)^{-1} Gamma_Q, proved as an identity of functions on the region \|\|A_Q\|\| < \|z\| where the Laurent series defining Q converges (both sides are rational, so this is the paper's rational continuation; the resolvent Neumann series is the reusable Analysis/ResolventNeumannSeries.lean); finrank_carrier and mcMillanDegree_eq_finrank_columnSpace = eq:supp-rational-degree (dimension = stabilized Hankel rank = dim of the Hankel column span), with mcMillanDegree_le_realization (every linear realization L A^n Gamma = M_n has at least that dimension) and canonical_isCyclic (source minimality); canonical_form_eq / negIndex_canonical_form (the Pontryagin form is the Hankel form and the negative index is its inertia, negIndex = largest dimension of a negative definite subspace); negSquares_toFun_eq_negIndex: the Nevanlinna kernel N_Q(z,w) = (Q(z)-Q(w)^*)/(z - conj w) (eq:supp-Nevanlinna-kernel) has exactly negIndex negative squares (sup of the negative inertia of finite kernel Grams) on every upper half-plane neighbourhood of infinity {Im z > 0, \|z\| > rho}, rho >= \|\|A_Q\|\|, and negSquares_canonical_transfer_eq on the whole upper half-plane part of the resolvent set of the realization; mechanism: inner_nevanlinnaKernel (kernel Gram = Pontryagin Gram of the resolvent-source vectors), negIndex_pullback_le (a compression cannot increase the index) and resolventSourceSpan_eq_top (resolvent-source span = power-cyclic span, via the limit -w(A-w)^{-1} -> 1). Uniqueness: PontryaginRealization.existsUnique_sourceFixing_unitary: any two source-cyclic finite Pontryagin realizations with the same Laurent coefficients are related by a unique linear isomorphism fixing the source, intertwining the state operators and preserving the indefinite forms (the source-fixing Pontryagin unitary). Scoped: 'minimal' is read as source-cyclic (the paper's source minimality; observability is automatic from nondegeneracy, futureRead_separating). |
| `thm:supp-pole-Hankel` | theorem | Pole--Hankel invariants | [`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.poleHankelInvariants`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.transfer_eq_principalPart_add_regularPart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.continuousAt_regularPart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.localCoeff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.principalPart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.regularPart`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.finrank_range_localHankelMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.finrank_range_localHankelMap_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.range_localCtrlMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.ker_localObsMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.gram_localKrylov_of_real`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.subNegIndex_rootSpace_eq_negInertia_localHankelGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.subPosIndex_rootSpace_eq_posInertia_localHankelGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.subNegIndex_add_subPosIndex_rootSpace_of_real`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.rieszProj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.rieszProj_A`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RootProjection.rootProj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/RootSubspaceProjection.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RootProjection.rootProj_comm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/RootSubspaceProjection.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RootProjection.isUnit_regularPencil`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/RootSubspaceProjection.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RootProjection.pow_sub_algebraMap_rootProj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/RootSubspaceProjection.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.subNegIndex_poleBlock_of_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelInvariants.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.negSquares_eq_sum_localIndex`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/PoleHankelInvariants.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NilpotentHankel.card_chains_ge_eq_finrank_sub`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/NilpotentHankelJordan.lean) | PROVED as NormalizedRationalHermitianData.poleHankelInvariants for the canonical minimal realization P = Q.canonical of a normalized rational Hermitian Q (its transfer function Gamma^+ (A - z)^{-1} Gamma is the rational continuation of Q, eq:supp-rational-realization). New infrastructure: Algebra/RootSubspaceProjection.lean (Riesz projection rootProj f mu onto the root subspace R_mu = maxGenEigenspace along the sum of the other root subspaces, for any endomorphism of a finite-dimensional space over an algebraically closed field: commutes with f, idempotent, (f - mu)^dim kills its range, and the regular pencil (f - mu)(1 - pi) + pi is invertible). Predictive/PoleHankelLocalRealization.lean: principal-part coefficients C_k = Gamma^* J (A - lam)^k pi_lam Gamma and the PRINCIPAL-PART DECOMPOSITION transfer_eq_principalPart_add_regularPart: for every z != lam in the resolvent set, Q(z) = -sum_{k < dim N} (z - lam)^{-k-1} C_k + R_lam(z), where R_lam(z) = Gamma^* J ((A - z)(1 - pi) + pi)^{-1} (1 - pi) Gamma is continuous at z = lam (continuousAt_regularPart), so the C_k are the principal part of Q at lam (for a non-pole lam they vanish). (P1) finrank_range_localHankelMap: local controllability (range_localCtrlMap) and observability (ker_localObsMap) of the local realization ((A - lam)\|R_lam, pi_lam Gamma, Gamma^* J\|R_lam) follow from source cyclicity and nondegeneracy, so rank [C_{i+j+s}]_{i,j < dim N} = rank ((A - lam)\|R_lam)^s for every shift s; s = 0 gives the local McMillan degree dim R_lam; by NilpotentHankel.card_chains_ge_eq_finrank_sub the differences of these ranks are the numbers of Jordan chains of each length. (P2) at a real lam the Gram of the local Krylov family is the principal-part Hankel Gram [<e_k, C_{i+j} e_l>] (gram_localKrylov_of_real), so neg(R_lam) = negInertia, pos(R_lam) = posInertia (subNegIndex/subPosIndex_rootSpace_eq_..._localHankelGram) and neg + pos = dim R_lam (nondegeneracy). (P3), (P4) as before (subNegIndex_poleBlock_of_pos, negSquares_eq_sum_localIndex). Scoped/disclosed: 'pole' is rendered as an arbitrary point lam (the statements are trivial when R_lam = 0); the uniqueness of a principal-part decomposition (that C_k is determined by Q alone, a textbook Laurent fact) is not separately formalised; the Jordan block sizes are rendered as the rank sequence of the nilpotent local state operator, and the count lemma takes the existence of a Jordan chain basis as a hypothesis (not in Mathlib); the Hankel operators use blocks of size dim N (any size >= the nilpotency index works). |

| PROVED (sorry-free, standard axioms only). New file Predictive/DescriptorWeierstrassForm.lean (1511 lines) closes the general (non-primary) side and assembles the corollary; bundled statement RenewalGeometry.cor_supp_all_rational. Setting: H, N finite-dimensional complex Hilbert spaces (the paper's finite setting); a regular Pontryagin descriptor realization is DescriptorRealization (E, A, Gamma, J) with regular pencil; a finite rational Hermitian dynamic function is FiniteRationalHermitianData Q = P + Q_0 (eq:supp-rational-split). (1) WEIERSTRASS REDUCTION: for any regular D and any real regular point t_0 (exists_real_regular), with T = E (A - t_0 E)^{-1}, pi_0 the Riesz projection onto the root subspace R_0 of T at 0 and W its root complement, the Weierstrass multiplier M = (1 + t_0 T) pi_0 + T (1 - pi_0) is an invertible J-self-adjoint operator commuting with T and pi_0 (weierstrassMul, isUnit_weierstrassMul, J_mul_weierstrassMul; pi_0 is J-self-adjoint, J_mul_rootProjL); the infinity block is the compression of (T M^{-1}, pi_0 Gamma, J M^{-1}) to R_0 (nilpotent, weierstrassInf_A_pow_finrank) and the finite block the compression of ((1 + t_0 T) M^{-1}, (1 - pi_0) Gamma, J M^{-1}) to W (PontryaginRealization.compress: compression of a Gram operator to a subspace on which its form is nondegenerate, compressGram_isUnit); D.weierstrassForm is the resulting PrimaryDescriptor and isSourceFixingUnitary_weierstrass verifies IsSourceFixingUnitary (D.rightMul (K M^{-1})) (D.weierstrassForm).toDescriptor S with S x = (pi_0 x, x - pi_0 x) (primaryEquiv), exactly as planned. weierstrassForm_transfer: the primary form has the same transfer function as D at every regular point (a source-fixing unitary preserves the transfer function, IsSourceFixingUnitary.transfer_eq). (2) MINIMALITY of general descriptor realizations: DescriptorRealization.IsMinimal := the resolvent-source vectors (A - zE)^{-1} Gamma h over the regular points z span the state space (the paper's source minimality of thm:supp-complete-rational-Krein). It is invariant under right multiplication (isMinimal_rightMul_iff) and source-fixing unitaries (IsSourceFixingUnitary.isMinimal, .symm), and on a primary form it is exactly PrimaryDescriptor.IsMinimal, i.e. both blocks source-cyclic (toDescriptor_isMinimal_iff; the Laurent/Vandermonde step is inlL_A_pow_Gamma_mem_resolventSourceSpan via coeff_eq_zero_of_tendsto_zero for the polynomial part and the real-ray resolvent limit Gamma_mem_resolventSourceSpan_real / resolventSourceSpan_realRay_eq_top for the strictly proper part). Hence D.IsMinimal <-> (D.weierstrassForm t_0).IsMinimal (isMinimal_iff_weierstrassForm_isMinimal). (3) THE EQUIVALENCE eq:supp-all-rational-equivalence: existence: Q.descriptor (primary direct sum of the infinity chain of P and the canonical realization of Q_0) is a minimal regular descriptor realization of Q (FiniteRationalHermitianData.descriptor_isMinimal, eventually_descriptor_transfer_eq_toFun); every minimal regular D realizes the finite rational Hermitian datum D.toData (eventually_toData_toFun_eq_transfer), whose coefficients are determined by the transfer function (toData_coeff_eq_of_transfer_eventuallyEq); uniqueness: a minimal D realizing Q is equivalent to Q.descriptor by reduction to Weierstrass primary form followed by a UNIQUE source-fixing descriptor Pontryagin unitary (existsUnique_sourceFixing_unitary_weierstrassForm_descriptor), and any two minimal realizations with the same dynamic function have Weierstrass forms related by a unique source-fixing unitary (existsUnique_sourceFixing_unitary_weierstrassForm). Dimension: every regular realization of Q has dim N >= McM(P) + McM(Q_0) (mcMillanDegree_add_le_finrank) and every minimal one has dim N = McM(P) + McM(Q_0) (finrank_eq_of_isMinimal): the minimal realizations attain the minimum state dimension. DISCLOSED RENDERINGS: (a) 'same dynamic function' is rendered as equality of transfer functions for all large real t (a neighbourhood of infinity on the real axis; rational functions agreeing there agree everywhere); (b) the paper's 'unique descriptor equivalence' between non-primary realizations is read as reduction to Weierstrass primary form (the right multiplication (E, A, Gamma, J) -> (EV, AV, Gamma, JV) by the J-self-adjoint unit V = (A - t_0 E)^{-1} M^{-1}, which preserves the transfer function, followed by the source-fixing unitary x -> (pi_0 x, x - pi_0 x)) followed by the unique source-fixing unitary between the primary forms; the primary form and D.toData depend on the chosen real regular point t_0, but the Laurent coefficients do not; (c) the negative-index part of 'attains both minimum ... indices' is not formalised here (the index statements are the separately ledgered thm:supp-complete-rational-Krein / thm:supp-pole-Hankel results); (d) minimality is source minimality (resolvent-source span = whole space), as in the paper's proof of thm:supp-complete-rational-Krein. |
| `prop:extension-indefinite-compression` | proposition | Negative-square index under effective-space compression | aKernelCompressionDegree.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.compress`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/NevanlinnaKernelCompression.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.nevanlinnaKernel_compress`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/NevanlinnaKernelCompression.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.kernelGram_compress`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/NevanlinnaKernelCompression.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.PontryaginRealization.negSquares_compress_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/NevanlinnaKernelCompression.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.negSquares_compress_toFun_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/NevanlinnaKernelCompression.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.mcMillanDegree_le_realization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData.mcMillanDegree`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalKreinCanonicalRealization.lean) | Proved (extension_indefinite_compression) for a normalized rational Hermitian Q on a finite-dimensional H (NormalizedRationalHermitianData) and any bounded V : H' -> H with H' finite-dimensional (the paper's isometry hypothesis is not needed; finite-dimensionality of H' is implicit in the paper's finite effective spaces). (1) Q_V is again normalized rational Hermitian: NormalizedRationalHermitianData.compress has coefficients V* M_n V (Hermitian; finite Hankel rank via finiteDimensional_hankelState_of_realization from the realization on the canonical state space of Q with source Gamma V and output V* Gamma^+, exactly the paper's proof), and compress_toFun_eq: Q_V(z) = V* Q(z) V on the Laurent region \|\|z\|\| > \|\|A_Q\|\|. (2) Index clause: negIndex [.,.]_{Q_V} <= negIndex [.,.]_Q (negIndex_compress_le; negIndex equals the number of negative squares of the Nevanlinna kernel on every upper half-plane neighbourhood of infinity, negSquares_toFun_eq_negIndex), and the direct kernel form negSquares(N_{V*QV}) <= negSquares(N_Q) on upperNeighbourhood rho, rho >= \|\|A_Q\|\| (kernel Gram congruence, negSquares_compress_le). (3) Degree clause: mcMillanDegree Q_V <= mcMillanDegree Q (mcMillanDegree_compress_le), where mcMillanDegree is the minimal finite realization dimension (lower bound mcMillanDegree_le_realization, attained by the canonical realization). (4) Composition: (Q.compress V1).compress V2 = Q.compress (V1 o V2) and V2*(V1* Q V1)V2 = (V1V2)* Q (V1V2) as functions (compress_compress). |

[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.CorrelationSpectrahedron`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.GaugeGroup`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.gaugeAct`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.gaugeAct_mem_corr`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.stabilizer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.IsJointRealization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.gram_mem_corr_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.exists_realization_of_mem_corr`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.finrank_range_eq_rank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.rank_le_card_of_realization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.SourceFixingUnitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.exists_unique_range_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.FrameUnitaryEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.frameUnitaryEquivalent_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.mem_stabilizer_iff_residual`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.joint_gram_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean) | Proved in finite dimensions with E_a = C^{n a} (index types in Type, as required by the reused repo lemma source_kernel_realization_minimal_unique) and X = Sigma a, n a. Corr_X = {K psd, K_aa = I} (CorrelationSpectrahedron); G_X = Pi_a U(n a) acting by block congruence K -> D(g)^H K D(g) (gaugeAct), which preserves Corr_X (block_gaugeAct, gaugeAct_mem_corr); Stab(K) is a Subgroup (stabilizer). A joint realization of K on a common space C^C is S : Matrix C X with S^H S = K (columns over block a = images of the marginal basis), its realization space Ran S. Proved: the Gram of a realization lies in Corr_X iff its marginal blocks are identities (isometric V_a); every K in Corr_X is realized (by sqrt K) with rank = rank K; every realization's Ran S has dimension exactly rank K and rank K <= dim of the common space (rank K is the minimum joint realization dimension, attained by restricting to Ran S); two realizations are equivalent up to a marginal frame change g and a source-fixing isometric identification of the realization spaces iff their Grams lie in the same G_X-orbit (frameUnitaryEquivalent_iff; the identification is unique, exists_unique_range_unitary, via the repo's joint_source_unique_range_unitary); for a realization S of K, a frame change g is implemented by such a unitary on Ran S iff g in Stab(K) (mem_stabilizer_iff_residual). Modelling disclosure: 'unitary on the common realization space' is formalised as a source-fixing linear equivalence between the ranges preserving the ambient inner product (the repo's convention), and 'minimal' as restriction to Ran S. |
| `cth:supp-pairwise-not-global` | proposition | Pairwise positivity need not give a joint realization | [`RenewalGeometry.pairwiseCounterexampleGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PairwiseGramNotGlobalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairwiseCounterexampleGram_mulVec_ones`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PairwiseGramNotGlobalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairwiseCounterexampleGram_hasEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PairwiseGramNotGlobalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairwiseCounterexampleGram_not_posSemidef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PairwiseGramNotGlobalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairwiseCounterexampleBlock_posDef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PairwiseGramNotGlobalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairwiseCounterexampleGram_principal_block_posDef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/PairwiseGramNotGlobalExact.lean) | The explicit 3×3 real matrix M = !![1,-3/4,-3/4; -3/4,1,-3/4; -3/4,-3/4,1]: M *ᵥ (1,1,1) = (-1/2)•(1,1,1), hence Module.End.HasEigenvalue (Matrix.toLin' M) (-1/2); M is not Matrix.PosSemidef (quadratic form -3/2 on (1,1,1)); and for every i ≠ j in Fin 3 the principal block M.submatrix ![i,j] ![i,j] is Matrix.PosDef (all blocks equal !![1,-3/4;-3/4,1], whose form is (x₀ - 3x₁/4)² + 7x₁²/16). So pairwise-admissible Gram blocks need not assemble into a positive joint Gram operator. |
| `thm:supp-finite-exhaustion` | theorem | Finite stratified fibre in the fully reduced coordinate model | [`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.instMulActionGaugeGroupCorr`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.mem_orbit_iff_gaugeAct`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.mem_stabilizer_iff_jointGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.FullyReducedRealization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.RealizationSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.gramMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.gramFibreEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.StratifiedQuotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.stratifiedExhaustion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.stratifiedExhaustion_fst`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StratifiedInverseFibre.finite_stratified_exhaustion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StratifiedInverseFibreExhaustionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EquivariantOrbit.EquivariantMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/EquivariantOrbitDecomposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EquivariantOrbit.EquivariantMap.fibre`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/EquivariantOrbitDecomposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EquivariantOrbit.EquivariantMap.orbitDecomposition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/EquivariantOrbitDecomposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EquivariantOrbit.EquivariantMap.orbitDecomposition_fst`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/EquivariantOrbitDecomposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EquivariantOrbit.orbitRelQuotientCongr`](../../RenewalGeometry/Algebra/EquivariantOrbitDecomposition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.JointGram.joint_gram_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/JointGramClassificationExact.lean) | Proved in the reformulation the paper's proof establishes. A fully reduced finite realization of the completed target is rendered by its retained data (FullyReducedRealization): a joint Gram point K in Corr_X (thm:supp-common-source, JointGram.CorrelationSpectrahedron) together with points of the five retained fibres J_X x M_X x P_X x C_X^cal x R_X, which enter as sets with an action of the gauge group G_X = Prod_a U(E_a); the gauge group acts on Corr_X by the block congruence of thm:supp-common-source (instMulActionGaugeGroupCorr; gaugeAct is a right action, composed with inversion to a left action with the same orbits, mem_orbit_iff_gaugeAct, and the same stabilizers, which are the residual gauge groups Stab_{G_X}(K) of thm:supp-common-source, mem_stabilizer_iff_jointGram). Real_X^{sharp,min}(S) is defined as the set of fully reduced realizations modulo simultaneous gauge equivalence (RealizationSpace, the orbit space). The theorem finite_stratified_exhaustion is eq:supp-finite-fibre: an explicit bijection stratifiedExhaustion : Real_X^{sharp,min}(S) = Sigma_{[K] in Corr_X/G_X} (J_X x M_X x P_X x C_X^cal x R_X)/Stab_{G_X}(K) sending the class of a realization into the stratum of the gauge orbit of its joint Gram point (stratifiedExhaustion_fst), with the strata indexed by the block-congruence orbits and each stratum quotiented by its residual gauge group; the quotient is stratified, not a product, exactly as the paper says. The general mechanism is the new reusable infrastructure Algebra/EquivariantOrbitDecomposition.lean: for a G-equivariant map pi : X -> Y, X/G = Sigma_{omega in Y/G} pi^{-1}(y_omega)/Stab(y_omega) (EquivariantMap.orbitDecomposition, with the fibre carrying the stabilizer action and orbitRelQuotientCongr transporting orbit spaces along equivariant bijections). Disclosures: the five factor classifications that the paper composes (graph inverse theorem for J_X = thm:supp-graph-realization, positive/Pontryagin memory classification for M_X = thm:supp-Naimark-realization and thm:supp-complete-rational-Krein, marked bundle descent for R_X = thm:supp-marked-bundle, now conditional_interface only for its spanning-tree chord normal form, which is not used since R_X is by definition the marked monodromy representation space) are cited, not re-derived: the factors are G_X-sets here, and the full-reduction hypotheses (null quotients taken, minimal Gram blocks, the finite separating family with trivial common kernel) are what justifies taking the retained data as the realization set; they are not themselves formalised, so the left side is defined as the orbit space of the retained data rather than derived from a Grand-tensor realization notion. The descriptor (pencil) part of the memory factor (cor:supp-all-rational, now proved) does not affect this statement either. |
