FiniteStageSystem.fibre_pruning`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/CompactFiniteStageSystem.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EquivariantInverseSystem.limitQuotientEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Topology/InverseLimitOrbitQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.EquivariantInverseSystem.limitQuotientMap_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Topology/InverseLimitOrbitQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InverseSystem.sectionsProdEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Topology/InverseLimitOrbitQuotient.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InverseSystem.compactSpace_sections`](../../RenewalGeometry/Topology/FiniteInverseSystemPruning.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InverseSystem.sectionsHomeomorphStable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Topology/FiniteInverseSystemPruning.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InverseSystem.sectionsEquivStable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Topology/FiniteInverseSystemStableImage.lean) | Proved from the finite-stage input data of def:supp-compact-fibre-system; nothing assumes that threads are realized. New file Spectralization/CompactFiniteStageRealization.lean. CompactFiniteStageData holds only stage data: screens P_n up to I; compact gauge groups and the six compact factors (the compactified memory factor is the weak-* compact tight memory set); continuous restriction maps; stage symmetric pseudo-resolvents R_n(z) on P_n H that converge strongly with an injective limit; compatible, increasing, finite-trace normal stage weights; and separating families of linear functionals on the space Q of additional retained directions, with trivial common kernel. The retained completed inverse fibre now has a concrete meaning. CompletedRealization consists of: a self-adjoint operator whose resolvents are the strong limits of the stage resolvents; a normal weight with tau = sup_n tau_n; a compatible thread of strata; and an additional direction that no finite-stage functional displays. Every limiting field of the old CompactFiniteStageSystem is now DERIVED (toSystem). The limit resolvent is the strong limit, bounded by Banach-Steinhaus. Its resolvent identity and adjoint symmetry pass to the limit using the automatic bound \|\|R(z)\|\| <= \|Im z\|^-1. Dense range follows from injectivity. The semifinite weight is the supremum. Retained := CompletedRealization. The realization step is proved: realize and tight build the operator with the new pseudo-resolvent theorem. That theorem says an injective symmetric pseudo-resolvent is the resolvent of a unique self-adjoint operator. The operator has domain range R(i) and A = R(i)^-1 + i; it is symmetric, A - z has bounded two-sided inverses, and A is IsSelfAdjoint in Mathlib's LinearPMap sense. The weight comes from NormalWeight.iSupWeight: additivity by monotone sums, normality by exchanging suprema, semifiniteness from compatibility. Injectivity is also proved. The operator is unique because the resolvent determines the generator (ext_of_resolvent); the weight is fixed by the sup formula; the additional direction vanishes by cofinal separation (extra_eq_zero). semifinite_exhaustion_ofData gives: the boxed isomorphism R_inf ~ varprojlim F_n^inf, compatible with the coordinates; the limit is nonempty and compact; there is no additional coordinate; the physical part; strong convergence of the stage resolvents to the resolvents of a unique self-adjoint operator; and the stage weights increasing to a normal semifinite weight. On factor-preserving strata, componentwiseCompletedEquiv gives the componentwise form. exists_factorThread realizes threads by threads of the six compact factors, unique up to the limit gauge group. Non-vacuity: exampleData on H = C has operator 'multiplication by 1', the vector weight, a two-point current factor and Q = C. Its completed fibre has two distinct points. Disclosed renderings: (i) memory tightness is encoded as compactness of the compactified memory factor, as in the definition; (ii) normality is sequential (sigma-normal); (iii) stage weights are assumed normal, which is what finite-trace weights are; (iv) the coordinate part of a completed realization is the compatible thread of strata itself, which is the paper's 'indefinitely extendible compact threads'; (v) the non-vacuity witness is finite-dimensional. All new declarations use only propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound. |

| Encoded by OperationalGrandTensor.GrandTensor.ofProcess X, built from a finite operational process model (repair of audit defect (b): system and gen are no longer independent data). FiniteOperationalProcess: over a typed event grammar (branch interventions a at slot `slot a` with Kraus vectors on H_out (x) H_in), the complete k-slot intervention tables for every k (multilinear functionals of the slot Choi matrices) together with the comb conditions on their reconstructed tensors (the paper's finite operational assumptions). system := X.predictiveSystem, the conditional event law p(w\|h) = p(hw)/p(h) read off the tables (chain rule proved from the comb realization: eventProb_eq_zero_of_prefix), whose MinimalCarrier is Z^min_X. gen := histGen of the canonical support-minimal realization: the branch letters pushed through the future-separated quotient A^proc / N^fut, with A^proc = range of the realization's typed-branch representation and N^fut = contextualFutureNullIdeal of the causal readouts (both q and q^* tested in every two-sided context). histAlgebraEquivQuotient / GrandTensor.ofProcess_spec: historyAlgebra gen is algebra-isomorphic to the Ideal quotient A^proc / N^fut (class [q] \|-> concrete representative, letters \|-> generators), i.e. A^hist is the paper's quotient (eq:ncg-history-algebra), presented concretely by compression onto the complement of the central support of N^fut (exists_centralSupport, exists_quotient_compression); A^hist is nonzero (histRank_pos). tau_reg and K_{X,r} are the derived fields regularTrace / wordGram (normalizedRegularTrace of A^hist, tau([v]^*[w])). The choice of realization does not matter: any prefix-support-minimal realization gives a star-isomorphic A^hist with the same word Grams (histAlgebra_realization_independent). Disclosed: (1) the generic container FiniteGrandTensor.GrandTensor still has free fields; the paper's object is GrandTensor.ofProcess; (2) the readout family R_X is rendered as the causal readouts at every cut eta (prepare the initial memory state, apply the represented history, discard the memory at eta: the declared future continuations); the 'minimal predictive record reads' are not adjoined as separate readouts; (3) uniform slot spaces and a common finite Kraus index type for all interventions (zero padding). Non-vacuity: qubitIdentityProcess example. | — |  |

Order-zero/first-order axioms are not imposed (they do not enter the definition of c_S). Non-vacuity: trivialMarkedFiniteSpectralTriple. | — |  |

[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.FiniteMarkedObject`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.FiniteUnitaryEquivalence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.CalibratedFiniteTriple`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.finiteReconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.MarkedCompactTriple`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.CompactUnitaryEquivalence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.CompactMarkedObject`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.MarkedCompactTriple.SpectralProjectionSequence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.MarkedCompactTriple.IsSpectralProjection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.MarkedCompactTriple.IsSymmetricSpectralProjection`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.MarkedCompactTriple.IsStrongCompression`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.MarkedCompactTriple.IsNormMultiplicativeCompression`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.MarkedCompactTriple.IsSpectrallyQuasidiagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.sqdProperty`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.SpecCptSqd`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.StrongCompressionObject`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.strongReconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.normCompressionProperty`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.NormCompressionObject`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReconstructionCategories.normReconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/ReconstructionCategories.lean) | New file ReconstructionCategories.lean. Spec_fin: FiniteMarkedObject A iota (marked finite spectral triples of def:marked-spectral-triple on finite carriers K : Type) with the Groupoid instance whose morphisms are FiniteUnitaryEquivalence (unitary U : C^K -> C^K' intertwining pi, D, the declared operator marks, the grading and the real structure with equal declared signs; absent marks absent on both sides). Spec_cpt^sep: CompactMarkedObject A iota = MarkedCompactTriple (an NCG.SpectralTriple, i.e. compact resolvent and the distinguished unital smooth *-subalgebra A_D = smoothAlgebra with bounded commutators, plus density of A_D rendered as operator-norm density of pi(A_D) in pi(A), optional grading/real structure compatible with D, declared bounded marks) on a SEPARABLE Hilbert space, with the Groupoid instance of CompactUnitaryEquivalence (unitary H ~= H' intertwining pi, mapping dom D onto dom D' with D'U = UD, preserving A_D, marks, grading, J). (C1) CalibratedFiniteTriple = triple + separating (injective) real linear commutant-coordinate map; record = (pi, gamma, J, marks, commutator derivation a -> [D, pi(a)], alpha(P_c D)); category with morphisms = unitary equivalences transporting the coordinate functionals (alpha'(U C U^H) = alpha(C), well defined by conj_mem_commutantSpace); reconstruction functor finiteReconstruction (D recovered by MarkedFiniteSpectralTriple.finite_calibrated_recovery). (C2) SpectralProjectionSequence: P_n = 1_{B_n}(D) (orthogonal projection onto the span of eigenvectors with eigenvalue in B_n), finite rank, P_n D = D P_n on dom D, increasing, P_n -> I strongly; IsStrongCompression = the four limits of eq:strong-compression-topology for a, b in A_D, every nonreal z (any bounded two-sided inverse of D - z) and every xi, with the commutator through its bounded extension, plus strong convergence of the declared bounded marks; StrongCompressionObject (also records finite commutant-coordinate functionals per level n) with morphisms intertwining the compression maps and transporting the functionals, and strongReconstruction. (C3) IsNormMultiplicativeCompression (eq:norm-multiplicative-compression), NormCompressionObject (full subcategory) and normReconstruction. eq:sqd: IsSpectrallyQuasidiagonal (finite-rank SYMMETRIC spectral projections 1_{[-R_n,R_n]}(D) increasing to I, commuting with D, \|\|[P_n, pi(a)]\|\| -> 0 on A_D, commuting with gamma and J when marked); SpecCptSqd = full subcategory. Renderings disclosed: the abstract algebra A carries no topology, so density of A_D is density of its image; resolvents and bounded commutator extensions enter through their defining equations; the per-level recorded commutant coordinates of compressed packets are kept as arbitrary recorded real-linear functionals (the paper does not specify them further). No non-vacuity witness is given for MarkedCompactTriple (constructing a compact-resolvent NCG.SpectralTriple is outside this batch); the finite level has trivialMarkedFiniteSpectralTriple. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. | — |  |

| Bundled structure StationaryRenewalGraph V: masses m_x > 0 with sum 1, rates k_xy >= 0 for x != y (diagonal normalized to 0, since the generator sum over y != x ignores it), the mass-stationarity equation sum_y (q_xy - q_yx) = 0 for q = m k, and connectedness (ConductanceConnected) of the positive support of c = (q+q^T)/2. Derived generator L f (x) = sum_{y != x} k_xy (f y - f x) (generator_eq_sum_erase), flux q, conductance c = symmetrizedConductance q, current j = (q - q^T)/2 with c symmetric, j antisymmetric, q = c + j and sum_y j_xy = 0. StationaryRenewalGraph.spectralization discharges the hypotheses of canonical_graph_spectralization (thm:supp-graph-square), so the Hodge-Dirac carriers dirac/differential/representation of the earlier encoding attach to every such graph. Scoped: real scalars, both orientations retained via V x V. | — |  |

[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.incidenceReal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.incidence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.currentPolytope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.closedCurrentPolytope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.currentPolytope_eq_fibre`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.currentPolytope_subset_closed`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.StationaryRenewalGraphFibre.SimpleOrientation.isStationaryCurrent_incidence_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StationaryRenewalGraphCurrentFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.GraphSpectralFibre`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.IsStationaryCurrent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.forwardFlux`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.reverseFlux`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibre.abs_lt_conductance_iff_flux_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/FiniteGraphSpectralUniversalityFibreExact.lean) | SimpleOrientation V E is a simple undirected graph presented by one orientation (tail, head) of each edge (no parallel edges, no antiparallel pair, hence no loops); incidenceReal is the real incidence matrix B : R^E -> R^V, B_{x e} = [tail e = x] - [head e = x], and incidence its complex copy. closedCurrentPolytope G c = {j \| B j = 0 and \|j_e\| <= c_e for all e} is the closed polytope Jbar(G,c) (eq:supp-current-polytope-closed) and currentPolytope G c = {j \| B j = 0 and \|j_e\| < c_e} is J(G,c) (eq:supp-current-polytope), with currentPolytope ⊆ closedCurrentPolytope. The revised characterization 'J(G,c) consists precisely of the currents with both directional fluxes c_e +- j_e strictly positive' is abs_lt_conductance_iff_flux_pos (\|j_e\| < c_e <-> 0 < forwardFlux = c_e + j_e and 0 < reverseFlux = c_e - j_e). currentPolytope_eq_fibre identifies J(G,c) with the complex-incidence GraphSpectralFibre via isStationaryCurrent_incidence_iff. Scoped: 'relative interior' is the definitional strict-inequality set (its topological identification with the relative interior of Jbar is not formalized); the arctanh-domain sentence is a remark. | — |  |

The Stieltjes self-energy Σ_μ(z) = ∫ (λ - z)⁻¹ dμ(λ) is Mathlib's vector-measure integral (VectorMeasure.integral) of the scalar function against μ paired through scalar multiplication ℂ × B(H) → B(H); the Euclidean memory kernel and the moments M_n are defined the same way. The atoms λ Q δ_λ are instantiated (atomic) and their self-energy computed. | — |  |

alGrading`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/StableSpinAtlasRealGradingExact.lean) | Encoded. (A1)-(A3) are CompatibleStableAtlas (unchanged). The optional real/grading clause is now AtlasRealGrading A: declared continuum actions (ContinuumRealGrading: antiunitary J : H ≃ₗᵢ⋆[ℂ] H with J^2 = eps, self-adjoint involutive grading gamma, both preserving dom(D-hat) with J D-hat = eps' D-hat J, gamma D-hat = -D-hat gamma, J gamma = eps'' gamma J, plus actions J_V, gamma_V on the first-order space V intertwined by the Sobolev embedding), finite antiunitaries J_h and gradings gamma_h with J_h D_h = eps' D_h J_h, gamma_h D_h = -D_h gamma_h, and the intertwining of both reconstructions W_h J_h = J W_h, W_h gamma_h = gamma W_h, I_h J_h = J_V I_h, I_h gamma_h = gamma_V I_h. The convergence clause is DERIVED, not a field: tendsto_embed_stageReal_adjoint / tendsto_embed_stageGrading_adjoint give W_h J_h W_h^* -> J and W_h gamma_h W_h^* -> gamma strongly from the intertwining and (A1); the finite KO signs J_h^2 = eps, gamma_h^2 = 1, J_h gamma_h = eps'' gamma_h J_h and self-adjointness gamma_h = W_h^* gamma W_h are also derived (W_h isometric). Non-vacuity (checklist k): exampleAtlas/exampleRealGrading, the doubled one-point model H = C^2, D-hat = D_h = sigma_1, gamma = sigma_3, J = complex conjugation (nontrivial: gamma anticommutes with D-hat). Disclosed surrogate (unchanged from the previous pass): no manifold/spinor-bundle objects; D-hat and H^1 are abstract parameters (SelfAdjointResolventData, compact embedding V -> H). | — |  |

easure.isSpectralMeasure_spectralProj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Spectralization/OperatorTailMeasureNaimarkRealizationExact.lean) | Construction encoded, with its defining properties proved. Analysis/OperatorMeasureL2Completion.lean builds, for any positive-semidefinite-valued vector measure μ with finite variation (PosOperatorMeasure; PositiveTailMeasure.toPosOperatorMeasure), the pre-Hilbert space PreL2 μ of bounded measurable H-valued functions with the semi-inner product ⟨f,g⟩_μ = ∫⟨f, dμ g⟩ (form; on indicators it is the paper's ∑⟨ξ_j, μ(E_j ∩ F_k) η_k⟩, form_indicator_const; positivity for simple functions by SimpleFunc.induction and for bounded measurable functions by dominated convergence, re_form_self_nonneg), the Hilbert space K_μ = L2 μ := Completion (SeparationQuotient (PreL2 μ)) (quotient by the null space, completed) with dense embedding L2.mk, the bounded multipliers L2.mul g (‖g f‖_μ ≤ sup\|g\| ‖f‖_μ from positivity applied to √(sup\|g\|²−\|g\|²) f; multiplicative, unital, additive, adjoint = conj), the projection-valued measure P_μ(E) = L2.proj (multiplication by 1_E: self-adjoint idempotents, P(E∩F)=P(E)P(F), P(univ)=1, finitely additive, strongly σ-additive hasSum_proj), and V_μ ξ = [1 ξ] = L2.embed with B_μ = V_μ^* (compressB). The unbounded operator C_μ = ∫λ dP_μ is not defined as a LinearPMap (no spectral theorem in Mathlib); it is represented by its bounded functional calculus: resolventOp z = multiplication by (λ−z)^{-1} (the resolvent (C_μ−z)^{-1}, z ∉ [0,∞)) and heatOp t = multiplication by e^{-tλ} (e^{-tC_μ}); these multipliers are set to 0 on the μ-null set (−∞,0] so that they are bounded. Disclosed surrogate: the pre-Hilbert space uses bounded measurable functions instead of simple functions (the simple functions are dense in it, mk_mem_closure_sourceSpan), which yields the same completion. | — |  |
| `def:supp-rational-Hermitian` | definition | Normalized rational Hermitian dynamic function | [`RenewalGeometry.NormalizedRationalHermitianData`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.laurentDynamicFunction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hermitianHankelColumn`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hermitianHankelColumnSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelForm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelForm_single_single`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelNull`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelNull_eq_ker`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelStateEquivColumnSpace`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelStateForm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelShift`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hankelStateForm_shift_pow_source`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/RationalHermitianHankelForm.lean) | Encoded: Q is represented by its Hermitian Laurent coefficients M_n at infinity (eq:supp-rational-Laurent; Q(z-bar)^* = Q(z) is coeff_symmetric, Q(infinity)=0 is strict properness of the expansion) with rationality encoded, per Kronecker, as finite Hankel rank (finiteRank); laurentDynamicFunction is the series -sum M_n z^{-n-1}. Hankel columns c_j(h), their span K_Q, the sesquilinear Hankel form with [c_i(u), c_j(v)] = <u, M_{i+j} v> (hankelForm_single_single), its null space, the quotient state space with the descended form (hankelStateForm), A_Q c_j = c_{j+1} (hankelShift, well-defined by hankelFormalShift_null_le) and Gamma_Q h = c_0(h) (hankelSource). Proved in addition: the null space equals the kernel of the formal-to-literal column map for Hermitian coefficients (hankelNull_eq_ker), so the quotient is canonically the literal column span (hankelStateEquivColumnSpace), and [A^i Gamma u, A^j Gamma v] = <u, M_{i+j} v>. Not encoded: the equivalence rational-function <-> finite Hankel rank (Kronecker) and the identification of the tsum with the rational function. | — |  |

| Encoded as CompactFiniteStageSystem H (CompactFiniteStageSystem.lean): increasing finite-rank orthogonal screens P_n (P_{n+1}P_n = P_n) with P_n -> I strongly; compact Hausdorff topological gauge groups G_n acting continuously on nonempty compact Hausdorff factor spaces Corr_n, Cur_n (closed current polytope), Mem_n, Mark_n, Cal_n, Mon_n; strata Stratum n = (Corr_n x ... x Mon_n)/G_n (diagonal action), proved compact Hausdorff and nonempty (a continuous compact-group action on a compact Hausdorff space is proper: properSMul_of_compactSpace, t2Space_orbitQuotient); the orbit-type decomposition F_n = Sigma_{[K] in Corr_n/G_n} (J x M x P x C x R)/Stab(K) as a bijection (orbitTypeDecomposition); continuous restriction maps with r_{n,n} = id and r_{l,n} = r_{m,n} r_{l,m}; stable images F_n^[m], F_n^infty (eq:supp-stable-fibre-images); compatible self-adjoint stage resolvent data R_n(z) on P_n H (adjoint symmetry, resolvent identity) converging strongly to the transported limiting family R(z) with resolvent identity, adjoint symmetry, injectivity and dense range; compatible finite-trace stage weights (additive, positively homogeneous, finite on positive operators, retained on P_n, increasing) with normal semifinite extension tau = sup_n tau_n, finite on every screen; retained completed data with compatible stage coordinates and finite separating families F_n with trivial common kernel after quotienting by the displayed coordinates. Non-vacuity witness CompactFiniteStage.trivialSystem (H = C). Rendering disclosed: the six factors are abstract compact Hausdorff G_n-spaces (the concrete closed current polytope, weak-* compact tight memory sets with trace-mass bounds, marking/calibration and monodromy carriers are instances of these parameters, not constructed here); finite-trace = finite rank; normality of the weights is rendered by the monotone-supremum extension (sigma-weak lower semicontinuity of an individual weight is not encoded); 'retained quasilocal directions' are rendered by the abstract type Retained with coordinates and functionals. | — |  |
